中文摘要：LLM智能体正日益被期望支持企业工作流，而这类任务通常涉及信息缺失、不确定性、反馈和长期权衡。然而，现有的企业和金融基准主要测试静态能力，如信息抽取、数值计算、领域知识和金融问答，导致交互式和长时程决策探索不足。为弥合这一差距，我们提出EnterpriseBench，一个评估LLM智能体从静态问答到动态决策整个谱系的基准。具体而言，EnterpriseBench将现有企业和金融问答数据集重组为一个按能力和难度标注的统一基础套件，并引入三个专业交互场景：Consulting，基于管理咨询风格的商业案例，通过多轮信息搜寻进行客户问题诊断；Beer Game，改编自经典的供应链管理模拟，用于延迟反馈下的库存控制；以及Enterprise Digital Twin，一个基于项目的业务模拟器，用于劳动力、风险和项目规划。在四种骨干模型下对九种智能体方法的实验表明，当前智能体在企业场景中尚未实现稳定、全面和跨任务的可靠性。这些结果表明，EnterpriseBench为评估LLM智能体在真实企业战略推理与决策中的表现提供了一个实用基准。

Original abstract: LLM agents are increasingly expected to support enterprise workflows, where tasks often involve missing information, uncertainty, feedback, and long-term trade-offs. However, existing enterprise and financial benchmarks mainly test static capabilities such as information extraction, numerical calculation, domain knowledge, and financial QA, leaving interactive and long-horizon decision-making underexplored. To bridge this gap, we introduce EnterpriseBench, a benchmark that evaluates LLM agents across this spectrum, from static question answering to dynamic decision-making. Specifically, EnterpriseBench reorganizes existing enterprise and financial QA datasets into a unified foundational suite annotated by capability and difficulty, and introduces three professional interactive settings: Consulting, based on management-consulting-style business cases for client problem diagnosis through multi-turn information seeking; the Beer Game, adapted from a classic supply-chain management simulation for inventory control under delayed feedback; and Enterprise Digital Twin, a project-based business simulator for workforce, risk, and project planning. Experiments with nine agent methods under four backbone models show that current agents have not yet achieved stable, comprehensive, and cross-task reliability in enterprise scenarios. These results show that EnterpriseBench provides a practical benchmark for evaluating LLM agents in realistic enterprise strategic reasoning and decision-making.

</details>


### [83] [KUPAS MASTER: Distilling the Tacit Expertise of Master Practitioners into Agent-Ready Experience Corpora](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37673)
*Changmian Wang,Yuchao Ma,Xuchao Lu,Chen Zhang,Ping Sun,Jiazheng Wang,Shan Wang,Xuanwen Chen,Yihe Sun,Ziyu Lu,Jianqiang Huang,Hongzhi Li,Ziqing Xia,Kaihua Tang,Xian-Sheng Hua,Qinghua Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Technical Report. Official website: https://lsf.kupasai.com/ Report homepage: https://tongjiai4e.github.io/KUPAS-MASTER-Report/

Selection reason: 将专家隐性经验蒸馏为agent可调用的经验库与技能，含输入/步骤/依赖/停止条件，与agent记忆和技能复用直接相关。

TL;DR: 提出 KUPAS MASTER 经验工程平台，通过九层认知语料构建将异构工作记录与从业者访谈转化为可追溯、可复用的经验资产和可调用技能；在20名随机从业者、1,576个源文件上处理出23,024条个体经验记录和13,113项组织资产；多领域评估中 KUPAS MASTER 智能体得分89.58，优于原始语料RAG的79.75和基础模型的70.63。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经验丰富的专业人士拥有大量隐性知识，如关键线索、判断理由和行动选择，但常规工作记录常遗漏这些内容，导致LLM智能体难以有效利用专业经验。因此需要一种将个体隐性经验转化为组织知识并增强智能体能力的工程化方法。

Method: 提出 KUPAS MASTER 经验工程平台，核心为九层认知语料构建。该平台将异构工作记录和从业者访谈转化为可追溯、可复用的经验语料；用情境、线索、判断、行动、边界、结果六个案例要素保留任务过程；沿九个抽取维度组织隐性经验，并存入规则、约束、最佳实践、负面示例、边缘案例、技能六个库；通过语义对齐、个体经验蒸馏、组织整合和交叉评审保留源证据、使用条件和未解决分歧；最终将资产打包为具有明确输入、步骤、依赖关系和停止条件的可调用技能，连接经验收集、任务执行与评估反馈。

Result: 使用来自20名随机抽取从业者的授权样本，平台将1,576个源文件处理为23,024条个体经验记录和13,113项组织资产。评估覆盖多个专业领域；在共同任务输入和评分标准下，基础模型、原始语料RAG和 KUPAS MASTER 智能体分别得分70.63、79.75和89.58，KUPAS MASTER 智能体在全部七个评分维度上均优于原始语料RAG。

Conclusion: KUPAS MASTER 平台提供了一条从个体隐性经验到组织知识及智能体能力的实用路径，表明通过对经验进行结构化、可追溯和可调用的工程化处理，可以提升LLM智能体在专业任务中的表现。

中文摘要：经验丰富的专业人士所知道的不仅仅是事实和结论。他们知道哪些线索重要、为什么某一判断合理，以及应采取何种行动。日常工作记录常常遗漏这种隐性知识，使大语言模型（LLM）智能体难以有效利用专业经验。我们提出 KUPAS MASTER，一个围绕九层认知语料构建的经验工程平台。它将异构工作记录和从业者访谈转化为可供智能体使用、可追溯且可复用的经验语料。六个案例要素保留任务过程：情境、线索、判断、行动、边界和结果。九层认知语料构建沿九个抽取维度组织隐性经验，并将所得资产存储于六个库中：规则、约束、最佳实践、负面示例、边缘案例和技能。语义对齐、个体经验蒸馏、组织整合和交叉评审保留源证据、使用条件和未解决的分歧。平台将这些资产打包为可调用技能，具有明确输入、步骤、依赖关系和停止条件，连接经验收集、任务执行和评估反馈。使用来自20名随机抽取的从业者的授权样本，平台将1,576个源文件处理为23,024条个体经验记录和13,113项组织资产。评估涵盖多个专业领域。在共同任务输入和评分标准下，基础模型、原始语料检索增强生成（RAG）和 KUPAS MASTER 智能体分别得分70.63、79.75和89.58。KUPAS MASTER 智能体在所有七个评分维度上均优于原始语料 RAG。该平台提供了一条从个体隐性经验到组织知识和智能体能力的实用路径。

Original abstract: Experienced professionals know more than just facts and conclusions. They know which cues matter, why a judgment is reasonable, and which action to take. Routine work records often leave out this tacit knowledge, making it difficult for Large Language Model (LLM) agents to use professional experience effectively. We introduce KUPAS MASTER, an experience engineering platform built around nine-layer cognitive corpus construction. It turns heterogeneous work records and practitioner interviews into traceable, reusable experience corpora for agents. Six case elements preserve the task process: context, cues, judgment, action, boundaries, and outcomes. Nine-layer cognitive corpus construction organizes tacit experience along nine extraction dimensions and stores the resulting assets in six libraries: rules, constraints, best practices, negative examples, corner cases, and skills. Semantic alignment, individual experience distillation, organizational consolidation, and cross-review preserve source evidence, conditions of use, and unresolved disagreements. The platform packages these assets into callable skills with explicit inputs, steps, dependencies, and stopping conditions, connecting experience collection to task execution and evaluation feedback. Using authorized samples from 20 randomly selected practitioners, the platform processed 1,576 source files into 23,024 individual experience records and 13,113 organizational assets. The evaluation spans multiple professional domains. Under common task inputs and scoring criteria, the base model, raw corpus retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), and KUPAS MASTER agent scored 70.63, 79.75, and 89.58, respectively. The KUPAS MASTER agent improved on raw-corpus RAG in all seven scoring dimensions. The platform provides a practical path from individual tacit experience to organizational knowledge and agent capabilities.

</details>


### [84] [EngiWorld: What Can Frontier Agents Deliver in Professional Engineering Environments?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37686)
*Hongcheng Gao,Hailong Qu,Yu Lei,Henghui Sun,Haoyang Li,Yipeng Wei,Naihao Xue,Xiaohan Yu,Zhuo Tao,Yihe Zang,Yajiao Wang,Jingyi Tang,Yi Li,Jingjing Zhou,Jie Luo,Bohan Zeng,Chengyu Shen,Hao Jiang,Chong Chen,Bowen Qu,Olive Huang,Zeqiang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Project page: https://engiworld.github.io

Selection reason: 首个专业工程环境下的agent基准，涵盖CAD/CAE等长程多软件任务与产物验证，对coded agent评测有迁移价值。

TL;DR: EngiWorld 是首个面向完整工程设计循环的基准，包含 1301 个专家策划任务，覆盖 CAD、CAE、CAM、BIM、EDA、3D 可视化 6 个领域和 26 个专业软件平台，提供 GUI/CLI 界面与 6 类任务；配套统一领域验证器进行以制品为中心的连续评估。七个前沿模型表现差距显著，最高 EngiScore 仅 44.3，多软件任务成功率 3.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用计算机使用智能体进展迅速，但专业工业工程自动化仍不可靠，原因在于工程工作流需要跨软件与设计阶段对几何、物理约束及依赖关系进行推理；因此需要能够衡量端到端专业工程软件操作能力的基准。

Method: 提出 EngiWorld 基准，围绕完整设计循环构建：1301 个专家策划任务，覆盖 CAD、CAE、CAM、BIM、EDA、3D 可视化 6 个领域和 26 个专业软件平台，提供 GUI 与 CLI 界面，含从软件选择到开放式任务的 6 类任务。同时提出以制品为中心的评估方法，基于统一领域验证器套件，程序化检查最终与中间制品的几何有效性、物理可行性和规则合规性，并对定量设计任务按规格达成度连续评分而非二元判定。

Result: 对七个前沿模型的评估显示显著能力差距：最强模型 EngiScore 仅 44.3，多软件尝试成功率仅 3.6%。

Conclusion: EngiWorld 为衡量端到端操作专业工程软件的智能体进展提供了首个严谨基础。

中文摘要：自主智能体在通用计算机使用方面已取得快速进展，但专业工业工程的可靠自动化仍遥不可及，因为工程工作流要求对几何与物理约束以及跨软件和设计阶段保持的依赖关系进行推理。我们提出 EngiWorld，这是首个围绕完整设计循环构建的基准：1,301 个由专家策划的任务，涵盖 6 个工程领域（CAD、CAE、CAM、BIM、EDA 和 3D 可视化）和 26 个专业软件平台，具有 GUI 和 CLI 两种界面，以及从软件选择到开放式任务的 6 种任务类型。我们进一步引入以制品为中心的评估方法，建立在统一的领域验证器套件之上，它以程序化方式检查最终和中间制品的几何有效性、物理可行性及规则合规性，并根据规格达成度对定量设计任务进行连续评分，而非二元成功。对七个前沿模型的评估揭示了巨大的能力差距：最强模型仅达到 44.3 的 EngiScore，且多软件尝试的成功率仅为 3.6%。EngiWorld 为衡量端到端操作专业工程软件的智能体进展提供了首个严谨基础。

Original abstract: Autonomous agents have made rapid progress in general-purpose computer use, but reliable automation of professional industrial engineering remains out of reach, as engineering workflows demand reasoning over geometric and physical constraints and dependencies preserved across software and design stages. We present EngiWorld, the first benchmark structured around the complete design loop: 1,301 expert-curated tasks spanning 6 engineering domains (CAD, CAE, CAM, BIM, EDA, and 3D visualization) and 26 professional software platforms, with both GUI and CLI interfaces and 6 task types ranging from software-selection to open-ended tasks. We further introduce an artifact-centric evaluation methodology built on a unified domain-verifier suite, which programmatically checks the geometric validity, physical feasibility, and rule compliance of final and intermediate artifacts, and scores quantitative design tasks continuously by specification attainment rather than binary success. Evaluation of seven frontier models reveals a substantial capability gap: the strongest model achieves an EngiScore of only 44.3, and just 3.6% of multi-software attempts succeed. EngiWorld provides the first rigorous foundation for measuring progress toward agents that operate professional engineering software end to end.

</details>


### [85] [Locating Answer-Correctness Signals in Frozen Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37700)
*Yuansen Liu,Yixuan Tang,Anthony Kum Hoe Tung*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 研究冻结LLM中答案正确性信号的可读性与融合，可用于agent自我验证与检索门控。

TL;DR: 该研究系统探查语言模型预测答案正确性的内部信号，发现正确性集中于答案片段，多种读出互补，融合在分布外最有效，并可用于门控检索控制器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有正确性探针多依赖单一信号族或层，分布偏移下脆弱；检索增强场景中专用检测器常关注段落忠实度，但证据无帮助或冲突时未必反映正确性。

Method: 在闭卷与带上下文设置下，搜索隐藏状态、token概率、残差流特征、注意力及融合，将读出作为预测性测量，跨两个骨干模型评估，并用于门控检索控制器。

Result: 正确性集中于答案片段，即使在检索下也能从答案token恢复；各信号族互补，融合在分布外提升最大；协议跨两个骨干有效，并可门控检索控制器。

Conclusion: 答案正确性可通过冻结模型单次前向传播的内部读出预测，应组合多信号族，尤其在分布外；该协议实用且可支持下游检索控制。

中文摘要：语言模型会暴露内部信号，这些信号能预测答案是否正确，并且可在冻结模型的单次前向传播中读取，无需额外生成。然而，现有探针往往固守单一信号族或层，在分布偏移下可能表现脆弱；在检索增强设置中，许多专用检测器转而针对段落忠实度，而当检索到的证据无帮助或相互冲突时，忠实度可能与正确性发生偏离。因此，我们追问：答案正确性在何处可读，哪些内部信号族承载它，以及它们应如何组合。我们搜索隐藏状态、token 概率、残差流特征、注意力及其融合，将选定的读出视为一种预测性测量，而非机制性定位。我们分别在闭卷和带上下文设置中运行该分析，因为上下文会改变哪些读出具有信息量。一种一致的解剖结构浮现出来：正确性集中于答案片段，即使在检索条件下也可从答案 token 中恢复；各信号族以互补方式承载正确性，因此融合它们最有帮助的场景是分布外，此时单一信号最弱。该协议在两个骨干模型上均有效，并作为一个下游用途对检索控制器进行门控。

Original abstract: Language models expose internal signals that predict whether an answer is correct, readable from a single forward pass of a frozen model without additional generations. Yet existing probes often commit to one signal family or layer and can be brittle under distribution shift; in retrieval-augmented settings, many specialized detectors instead target passage faithfulness, which can diverge from correctness when retrieved evidence is unhelpful or conflicting. We therefore ask where answer correctness is readable, which internal signal families carry it, and how they should be combined. We search over hidden states, token probabilities, residual-stream features, attention, and their fusion, treating the selected readouts as a predictive measurement rather than a mechanistic localization. We run this analysis separately in closed-book and with-context settings, since context can change which readouts are informative. A consistent anatomy emerges: correctness concentrates in the answer span, recovered from the answer tokens even under retrieval, and the families carry it complementarily, so fusing them helps most out of distribution, where a single signal is weakest. The protocol is effective across two backbones and gates a retrieval controller as one downstream use.

</details>


### [86] [Context Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37725)
*Rulin Shao,Shannon Zejiang Shen,Junjie Oscar Yin,Yuetai Li,Minheng Wang,Hamish Ivison,Radha Poovendran,Nathan Lambert,Teng Xiao,Mike Lewis,Wen-tau Yih,Luke Zettlemoyer,Pang Wei Koh*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 提出模型原生管理上下文文件的语言模型，支持多agent上下文共享与技能优化，与agent harness设计高度相关。

TL;DR: 提出上下文语言模型（CLM），让模型把上下文作为文件原生管理；零样本构建在多项任务上超越 SOTA 上下文管理策略，并支持指令引导、在线强化学习和高效服务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上下文管理通常依赖外部框架控制，模型难以自主学习应在上下文中保留什么；需要将上下文管理内化为模型行为，并支持多智能体场景。

Method: 将上下文视为文件并允许模型无限制更新；用现有模型零样本构建 CLM；通过标准技能优化循环演化自然语言指令进行引导；引入在线强化学习方法；为 CLM 服务协同设计 Suffix Cache Reuse。

Result: BrowseComp-Plus 上准确率高 11.4% 且 FLOPs 少 21.5%；12 小时 EdgeBench 上得分高 5% 且 FLOPs 少 59%；24 小时多仓库智能体集群任务同算力下改进高 65%；指令引导使上下文管理留出集准确率最多提升 35.9 个百分点；在线 RL 使 Qwen3.5-9B 在 BrowseComp-Plus 上性能提升 47.6% 且 FLOPs 少 12%；Suffix Cache Reuse 在匹配性能下相对标准 SGLang 降低服务器端计算 35%。

Conclusion: CLM 将上下文管理从外部控制转向模型内在行为，既支持上下文内学习也支持参数化学习，并在准确率、计算效率和推理服务上取得一致收益。

中文摘要：我们提出上下文语言模型（Context Language Models, CLMs），即原生管理自身上下文的一类语言模型。我们通过将上下文视为文件，并允许模型对该文件进行无限制更新来实现这一点。这使模型能够学习上下文中最需要保留的内容，并自然扩展到多智能体系统，在该系统中多个智能体上下文可作为文件共存。使用现有模型以零样本方式构建 CLMs，在多种任务上优于 SOTA 上下文管理策略：在 BrowseComp-Plus 上准确率提高 11.4%，FLOPs 减少 21.5%；在 12 小时 EdgeBench 上得分提高 5%，FLOPs 减少 59%；在 24 小时多仓库智能体集群任务上，在相同计算量下改进幅度高 65%。此外，通过将上下文管理从外部框架控制转变为模型内在行为，CLMs 自然支持上下文管理策略的上下文内学习与参数化学习。我们表明，可以通过经标准技能优化循环演化出的自然语言指令来引导 CLMs，在上下文管理任务上将留出集准确率最多提高 35.9 个百分点，同时减少计算量。我们还为 CLMs 引入一种在线强化学习方法，在 BrowseComp-Plus 上将 Qwen3.5-9B 的性能提高 47.6%，同时减少 12% 的 FLOPs。最后，我们为 CLM 服务协同设计后缀缓存复用（Suffix Cache Reuse），在匹配性能下，相对于标准 SGLang 进一步将服务器端计算量减少 35%。

Original abstract: We introduce Context Language Models (CLMs), language models that natively manage their own context. We implement this by treating the context as a file and allowing the model to make unrestricted updates to this file. This allows the model to learn what is most important to maintain in context, and naturally extends to multi-agent systems where multiple agent contexts coexist as files. Building CLMs zero-shot with existing models outperforms SOTA context management strategies across a variety of tasks: 11.4% higher accuracy with 21.5% fewer FLOPs on BrowseComp-Plus, 5% higher scores with 59% fewer FLOPs on 12-hour EdgeBench, and 65% greater improvement with the same compute on a 24-hour multi-repository agent-swarm task. Moreover, by shifting context management from external harness control to intrinsic model behavior, CLMs naturally enable both in-context and parametric learning of context-management strategies. We show that CLMs can be steered with natural-language instructions evolved through a standard skill-optimization loop, improving held-out accuracy by up to 35.9 points on a context-management task while reducing compute. We also introduce an online reinforcement learning method for CLMs, improving Qwen3.5-9B performance on BrowseComp-Plus by 47.6% while using 12% fewer FLOPs. Finally, we co-design Suffix Cache Reuse for CLM serving, further reducing server-side compute by 35% relative to standard SGLang at matched performance.

</details>


### [87] [ContextRender: From Execution Dependencies to Agent Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37743)
*Savini Kashmira,Jayanaka L. Dantanarayana,Lingjia Tang,Jason Mars*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 基于执行依赖图管理agent上下文，利用工具结果复用信号选择历史，直接提升长程agent效率。

TL;DR: ContextRender 通过执行依赖图和 Tool-Flow Analysis 的 observed reuse 信号，在固定历史预算内选择并保留工具结果，在降低推理成本的同时保持或提升长时程任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程 LLM 智能体需要复用历史工具结果；全量历史开销大，压缩又易丢失后续所需信息，现有方法未充分利用早期工具结果在后续执行中的使用关系。

Method: 提出 ContextRender：维护持久执行依赖图；用 Tool-Flow Analysis 追踪后续操作对早期工具结果的复用，得到 observed reuse 信号；渲染器融合该信号、近因性和语义相关性，在固定历史预算内选取结果并保留被省略结果。

Result: 在 AppWorld 与 8-objective QA 上，使用三种执行模型，6K 历史预算下优于评估的上下文管理基线；任务性能接近或高于 Full history，平均推理成本相对 Full history 降低 10.2%-32.2%；消融显示 observed reuse 提升任务性能和对后续复用结果的保留。

Conclusion: 基于执行依赖与观察到的复用的上下文管理，可在远低于最大上下文窗口的预算下有效支撑长时程智能体任务，兼顾性能与成本。

中文摘要：执行长时程任务的 LLM 智能体会累积后续步骤可能需要的工具结果。即使完整历史能够容纳在上下文窗口内，将完整历史传递给每次调用仍然代价高昂；而缩减历史则有遗漏所需信息的风险。现有上下文管理方法可能忽视较早的工具结果在后续执行中如何被使用，导致所需信息未被纳入上下文。我们提出 ContextRender，它通过一个持久的执行依赖图来管理上下文。我们开发了 Tool-Flow Analysis，用于追踪后续操作如何复用较早工具结果中的信息，并提供一种称为 observed reuse 的信号。一个渲染器将该信号与近因性和语义相关性结合，以在固定历史预算内选择结果，并保留被省略的结果以供后续使用。在 AppWorld 和 8-objective QA 上，使用三种执行模型，ContextRender 在 6K 历史预算下优于所评估的上下文管理基线，该预算远低于模型的最大上下文窗口。在该预算内，它取得接近或高于传递完整历史的任务性能，同时相对于 Full history 将平均推理成本降低 10.2%-32.2%。消融实验表明，observed reuse 提升了任务性能以及对后续被复用结果的保留。

Original abstract: LLM agents performing long-horizon tasks accumulate tool results that later steps may need. Passing the full history to every invocation is costly even when it fits within the context window, while reducing it risks omitting needed information. Existing context management methods can overlook how earlier tool results are used in subsequent execution, leaving needed information out of context. We introduce ContextRender, which manages context through a persistent graph of execution dependencies. We develop Tool-Flow Analysis to track how later operations reuse information from earlier tool results, providing a signal called observed reuse. A renderer combines this signal with recency and semantic relevance to select results within a fixed history budget, retaining omitted results for later use. Across AppWorld and 8-objective QA with three execution models, ContextRender outperforms the evaluated context management baselines using a 6K history budget, well below the models' maximum context windows. Within this budget, it achieves task performance close to or above that of passing the full history while reducing mean inference cost by 10.2%-32.2% relative to Full history. Ablations show that observed reuse improves task performance and retention of results reused later.

</details>


### [88] [Mixture of Self-Improving Branches For Agent Harness Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37834)
*Haoyu Dong,Yuhang Zhou,Zihao Lin,Yifan Wu,Bo Peng,Mingyi Wang,Xiangjun Fan,Lizhu Zhang,Zhuokai Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 面向agent harness的递归自我改进与分支搜索，含SWE-bench等代码优化评测，与SemOpt直接相关。

TL;DR: 提出分支式自适应harness优化：各分支演化开发子集与提议策略，并用路由器选择分支头，从而避免单一搜索轨迹局部最优，在多个推理与编程基准上超越Meta-Harness。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Meta-Harness等RSI方法保留固定开发集和提议策略，使搜索沿单一轨迹演化，容易收敛到局部最优。

Method: 将搜索组织为多个分支，每个分支动态保留本分支领先harness更具优势的案例、删除所有分支领先harness均能解决的案例，并基于自身搜索历史更新提议策略；部署时用路由器按输入选择一个开发集选出的分支头。

Result: 相较Meta-Harness，在奥林匹克级数学推理上相对提升34.8%，Terminal-Bench 2.0提升11.6%，SWE-bench Lite提升3.8%，且harness选择与路由器配置仅使用开发数据。

Conclusion: 演化分支目标与提议策略可产生互补harness，路由器无需测试结果即可结合其优势，提升RSI式harness优化的效果。

中文摘要：Harness优化为递归自我改进（RSI）提供了一个实用场景，其中智能体生成的修改通过执行反馈来影响后续变更。近期如Meta-Harness等工作通过迭代式代码生成与评估实现了这一过程，但仍保留固定的开发集和提议策略。这些约束将演化导向单一搜索轨迹，增加了收敛到局部最优的风险。我们通过将搜索组织为具有演化开发子集和提议策略的分支，使改进过程本身具备自适应性。每个分支保留由其更多领先harness解决、而非其他分支领先harness解决的开发案例，丢弃所有分支中每个领先harness都能解决的案例，并利用自身搜索历史修订其提议策略。为部署由此得到的互补harness，我们提出一个路由器，在执行前为每个新输入选择一个由开发集选出的分支头。在数学推理和智能体编程基准上，我们的系统相对于Meta-Harness取得如下相对提升：奥林匹克级数学推理34.8%、Terminal-Bench 2.0为11.6%、SWE-bench Lite为3.8%，且harness选择和路由器配置仅基于开发数据。这些结果表明，演化的分支目标和提议策略能够产生互补harness，路由器可在无法访问测试结果的情况下结合它们的优势。

Original abstract: Harness optimization provides a practical setting for recursive self-improvement (RSI), where agent-generated modifications inform subsequent changes through execution feedback. Recent work such as Meta-Harness implements this process through iterative code generation and evaluation, but retains a fixed development set and proposal policy. These constraints channel evolution along a single search trajectory, increasing the risk of converging to a local optimum. We make the improvement process itself adaptive by organizing search into branches with evolving development subsets and proposal policies. Each branch retains development cases solved by more of its leading harnesses than by those of other branches, drops cases solved by every leading harness across all branches, and revises its proposal policy using its own search history. To deploy the resulting complementary harnesses, we propose a router to select one development-selected branch head for each new input before execution. Across mathematical reasoning and agentic coding benchmarks, our system achieves relative improvements over Meta-Harness of 34.8% on Olympiad-level mathematical reasoning, 11.6% on Terminal-Bench 2.0, and 3.8% on SWE-bench Lite, with harness selection and router configuration based solely on development data. These results show that evolving branch objectives and proposal policies can yield complementary harnesses whose strengths a router combines without access to test outcomes.

</details>


### [89] [Guide, Then Let Go: Gap-Adaptive Teacher Scheduling for Sparse-Reward Agentic RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37898)
*Youling Huang,Tiankuo Xu,Jiaji Liu,Tong Zheng,Shuo Zhou,Shaotong Qi,Junchi Yao,Shiyang Liu,Hao Xu,Pengcheng Xu,Bo Huang,Hongyi Fu,Lin Lin*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 面向稀疏奖励长程agent的RL训练，提出按师生差距自适应调度蒸馏，可迁移到agent策略训练。

TL;DR: 提出 GATS，通过自适应调整 OPD 教师指导权重缓解长时程智能体强化学习的冷启动问题，在多个任务和师生配置上超越仅奖励 GRPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程强化学习通常依赖稀疏结果奖励，早期策略常无法完成任务，导致严重冷启动；OPD 可提供 token 级指导，但其收益取决于师生性能差距。

Method: 使用 on-policy distillation 在学生自身 rollout 上提供 token 级指导；提出 Gap-Adaptive Teacher Scheduling，将权重随师生性能差距自适应调整的 OPD 项加入学生 RL 目标：学生接近教师参考性能时逐步减少指导，达到后撤回指导。

Result: 在 ALFWorld、WebShop、ScienceWorld 的三种 Qwen2.5 师生配置中，GATS 均取得所比较方法中最高的平均成功率；在匹配学生 rollout 预算下，相较 reward-only GRPO 提升 4.37%-11.87%。

Conclusion: 教师指导主要对早期训练有效，GATS 可自适应利用甚至小于学生的任务训练教师来缓解冷启动并提升性能。

中文摘要：面向长时程智能体的强化学习通常依赖稀疏的基于结果的奖励。这会导致严重的冷启动问题，因为早期策略往往无法解决采样到的任务，从而几乎没有为学习留下有用的奖励信号。为缓解这一问题，我们使用同策略蒸馏（on-policy distillation, OPD）在学生自身的 rollout 上提供 token 级指导。我们发现，这种指导的收益取决于教师与学生之间的性能差距。当教师显著优于学生时，蒸馏有助于引导学生度过结果奖励几乎无法提供学习信号的早期训练阶段。然而，随着差距缩小并最终反转，继续蒸馏的收益会降低，甚至可能阻碍进一步改进。受此观察启发，我们提出差距自适应教师调度（Gap-Adaptive Teacher Scheduling, GATS），它通过一个 OPD 项增强学生的 RL 目标，该 OPD 项的权重会根据师生性能差距自适应调整。具体而言，GATS 在学生接近教师的参考性能时逐步减少教师指导，并在达到该参考性能后撤回指导。这使得 GATS 能够利用比学生更小的、经过任务训练的教师，因为教师指导主要是在早期训练阶段需要。在 ALFWorld、WebShop 和 ScienceWorld 上，使用三种 Qwen2.5 师生配置，GATS 在所有三种配置中取得了所比较方法中最高的平均成功率，在匹配学生 rollout 预算下，相较仅使用奖励的 GRPO 提升了 4.37%-11.87%。代码见 https://github.com/Ricardo-H/guide-then-let-go。

Original abstract: Reinforcement learning for long-horizon agents typically relies on sparse outcome-based rewards. This leads to a severe cold-start problem, as early-stage policies often fail to solve sampled tasks, leaving little useful reward signal for learning. To mitigate this problem, we use on-policy distillation (OPD) to provide token-level guidance on the student's own rollouts. We find that the benefit of this guidance depends on the performance gap between the teacher and the student. When the teacher substantially outperforms the student, distillation helps guide the student through the early training stage where outcome rewards provide little learning signal. As the gap narrows and eventually reverses, however, continued distillation becomes less beneficial and may hinder further improvement. Motivated by this observation, we propose Gap-Adaptive Teacher Scheduling (GATS), which augments the student's RL objective with an OPD term whose weight adapts to the teacher-student performance gap. Specifically, GATS gradually reduces teacher guidance as the student approaches the teacher's reference performance and withdraws it once that reference is reached. This enables GATS to leverage task-trained teachers smaller than the student, since teacher guidance is primarily needed during early training. Across ALFWorld, WebShop, and ScienceWorld with three Qwen2.5 teacher-student configurations, GATS achieves the highest average success rate among the compared methods in all three configurations, improving over reward-only GRPO by 4.37%-11.87% under matched student rollout budgets. Code is available at https://github.com/Ricardo-H/guide-then-let-go.

</details>


### [90] [Video-RSI: Recursive Self-Improvement of Video Understanding Agents via Harness Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37950)
*Bingjun Luo,Jialin Guo,Siqi Li*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: Video-RSI通过主动重访证据进化agent harness，实现递归自我改进与成本-准确率权衡，harness演化机制可直接迁移到coding agent。

TL;DR: Video-RSI 让视频理解智能体用自身语言模型递归修订执行框架；通过主动回看训练视频检验失败解释，并按准确率与视觉成本演化框架，最终在更少帧数下提升准确率并取得有竞争力的精度-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有执行轨迹只包含当前执行框架获取的证据，无法解决关于失败的竞争性解释，限制了智能体自我改进的基础。

Method: 提出 Video-RSI 递归自我改进框架：视频理解智能体用自身语言模型修订执行框架；通过主动视频调查，重新访问原始训练视频，以额外观察检验竞争性失败解释，使修订基于现有轨迹之外的证据；采用成本感知的执行框架演化，将诊断转化为可重用修订，并依据答案准确率和视觉成本决定保留哪些修订。

Result: 在视频理解基准的评估设置中，演化后的智能体提高了准确率，同时处理更少帧，并在准确率-效率权衡上相较于现有视频理解智能体取得有竞争力的表现。

Conclusion: 结果表明，视频理解智能体有潜力通过执行框架演化来改进自身的证据获取与使用。代码已在 https://github.com/bingjunluo/Video-RSI 提供。

中文摘要：视频理解智能体通过一个可执行的执行框架获取证据，该框架控制它们观察什么以及如何使用这些观察。然而，执行轨迹仅包含当前执行框架所获取的证据，使得对失败原因的竞争性解释无法解决，并限制了自我改进的基础。我们提出 Video-RSI，一种递归自我改进框架，其中视频理解智能体使用自身的语言模型来修订其执行框架。通过主动视频调查，模型重新访问原始训练视频，以额外观察来检验相互竞争的失败解释，从而将所提出的更改建立在现有轨迹之外的证据之上。成本感知的执行框架演化将这些诊断转化为可重用的修订，并通过同时考虑答案准确性和视觉成本来决定保留哪些修订。在视频理解基准上的评估设置中，演化后的智能体在提高准确率的同时处理更少的帧，并在准确率-效率权衡上相较于现有视频理解智能体取得有竞争力的表现。这些结果表明，视频理解智能体有潜力通过执行框架演化来改进自身的证据获取与使用。代码可在 https://github.com/bingjunluo/Video-RSI 获取。

Original abstract: Video understanding agents acquire evidence through an executable harness that controls what they observe and how they use those observations. However, execution traces contain only the evidence acquired by the current harness, leaving competing explanations for failure unresolved and limiting the basis for self-improvement. We introduce Video-RSI, a framework for recursive self-improvement in which a video understanding agent uses its own language model to revise its harness. Through active video investigation, the model revisits the original training videos to test competing failure explanations with additional observations, grounding proposed changes in evidence beyond the existing trace. Cost-aware harness evolution turns these diagnoses into reusable revisions and determines which revisions to retain by considering both answer accuracy and visual cost. Across our evaluation settings on video understanding benchmarks, the evolved agent improves accuracy while processing fewer frames and achieves competitive accuracy-efficiency trade-offs against existing video understanding agents. These results demonstrate the potential for video understanding agents to improve their own evidence acquisition and use through harness evolution. Code is available at https://github.com/bingjunluo/Video-RSI .

</details>


### [91] [Topological Coherence for Self-evolving Multi-agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37953)
*Sen Zhao,Ruiqi Kong,Zuyu Zhang,Lifeng Shen,Xinyu He,Xu Zhang,Qinghua Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: TOCOMAS提出拓扑一致的多agent自演化框架，联合优化agent、协作与记忆边界，并在SWE-Bench上验证，可迁移到多agent代码优化。

TL;DR: TOCOMAS通过以工具接口锚定任务图、构建可复用责任域，生成与依赖和画像一致的协作及边界调控的记忆可见性，并在在线自演化中仅保留满足结构约束且提升奖励的智能体、协作与记忆策略，在多个基准上提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂任务中责任、交接与记忆边界必须与任务依赖保持一致；现有联合优化智能体与通信结构的方法并不强制这种一致性，即缺少拓扑一致性。

Method: 提出TOCOMAS：以工具接口锚定任务图，将兼容任务节点组织为可复用责任域，推导依赖引发且受画像条件约束的协作与边界调控的记忆可见性；在线自演化时，对智能体、协作和记忆策略提出耦合变更，仅保留满足结构约束并提升评估奖励的候选。

Result: 在BBEH、WorkBench、SWE-Bench-Verified和CoMemBench上，TOCOMAS在不同主干模型上均优于基线；CoMemBench中在已验证进展、交接和记忆隔离上优于自演化基线。

Conclusion: 拓扑一致性是复杂多智能体任务有效性的关键要求，TOCOMAS通过结构约束下的耦合自演化实现责任、协作与记忆边界的一致，并带来稳定增益。

中文摘要：复杂任务本质上耦合了工作流结构、智能体责任、协作与记忆访问：任务区域界定责任与工具范围，跨区域依赖产生交接，归属边界界定私有记忆与选择性共享记忆。现有方法能够联合优化智能体结构与通信结构，然而这种优化本身并不要求责任、交接和记忆边界与任务依赖保持一致。我们将这一要求称为拓扑一致性。我们提出TOCOMAS，一种拓扑一致的多智能体系统。TOCOMAS将任务图建立在工具接口之上，将兼容的任务节点组织为可复用的责任域，并推导出依赖引发且受画像条件约束的协作，以及边界调控的记忆可见性。在线自演化过程中，TOCOMAS提出对智能体、协作和记忆策略的耦合变更，仅为后续任务保留满足结构约束并提升评估奖励的候选方案。在BBEH、WorkBench、SWE-Bench-Verified和CoMemBench上，TOCOMAS在不同主干模型上较基线提升了任务成功率。CoMemBench还显示，相较于自演化基线，在已验证进展、交接和记忆隔离方面取得增益。

Original abstract: Complex tasks inherently couple workflow structure, agent responsibility, collaboration, and memory access: task regions delimit responsibility and tool scope, cross-region dependencies give rise to handoffs, and ownership boundaries delimit private and selectively shared memory. Existing methods can jointly optimize agent and communication structures, yet such optimization does not by itself require responsibility, handoff, and memory boundaries to remain consistent with task dependencies. We term this requirement topological coherence. We introduce TOCOMAS, a Topology-Coherent Multi-Agent System. TOCOMAS grounds a task graph in tool interfaces, organizes compatible task nodes into reusable responsibility domains, and derives dependency-induced and profile-conditioned collaboration together with boundary-regulated memory visibility. During online self-evolution, TOCOMAS proposes coupled changes to agent, collaboration, and memory policies, retaining for subsequent tasks only candidates that satisfy structural constraints and improve evaluated reward. Across BBEH, WorkBench, SWE-Bench-Verified, and CoMemBench, TOCOMAS improves task success over baselines across backbones. CoMemBench also shows gains over the self-evolving baseline in verified progress, handoffs, and memory isolation.

</details>


### [92] [SelfSearch: Reward-Free Search for Self-Improving Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37968)
*Jungwoo Yang,Injin Kong,Yohan Jo*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: SelfSearch用历史自我改进轨迹做无奖励搜索，直接优化coding agent的harness、指令与工具，在SWE-bench/Terminal-Bench上提升且降本，与SemOpt高度相关。

TL;DR: SelfSearch让LLM智能体利用过往自我改进记录进行无奖励自我修改，在多种模型—基准设置中提升成功率并降低成本，以较低搜索开销达到与评估引导搜索相当的竞争性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于反复下游评估的智能体搜索成本高，且将搜索过程绑定到被评估任务上，因此需要一种不依赖下游奖励信号、可复用自我改进经验的方法。

Method: 提出SelfSearch：一种无奖励搜索过程，智能体根据先前自我改进回合的记录（包括推理、工具动作和结果）修改自身，从而同时改进任务求解与自我修改能力。

Result: 在六种模型—基准设置中均提升群体平均成功率；Terminal-Bench 2.1上单个智能体最多提升11.2个百分点；SWE-bench Multilingual上提升5.0个百分点，并在共同解决任务上降低38.5%执行成本；以4.03美元搜索成本生成harness，在公开九种harness对比设置下用DeepSeek V4 Flash解决82.0%的Terminal-Bench 2.1任务，与Codex持平。

Conclusion: 通过自我修改获得的经验可以提升智能体的下游能力与效率，并能在较低搜索成本下取得有竞争力的任务成功率。

中文摘要：LLM智能体在编码能力上的进步，使其能够检查并修改自身的指令、工具和执行流程。现有方法利用这一能力，通过反复的下游评估来搜索改进后的智能体，但这会带来高昂成本，并将搜索与被评估任务绑定。我们提出SelfSearch，一种无奖励搜索过程，其中智能体利用先前自我改进回合的记录来修改自身。这些记录包含早期修改尝试的推理、工具动作和结果，为改进任务求解和自我修改提供了具体经验。在搜索过程中没有下游奖励信号的情况下，SelfSearch在全部六种模型—基准设置中都相对于初始智能体提升了群体平均成功率，单个智能体在Terminal-Bench 2.1上最多提升11.2个百分点。在SWE-bench Multilingual上，一个智能体将成功率提升了5.0个百分点，同时在初始智能体和进化后智能体都解决的任务上，将执行成本降低了38.5%。SelfSearch以更低的搜索成本，取得了与评估引导搜索基线相当的任务成功率。仅用4.03美元的搜索成本，它就产生了一个harness，在公开的九种harness对比设置下，使用DeepSeek V4 Flash解决了82.0%的Terminal-Bench 2.1任务，与最高分的harness——Codex——持平。这些结果表明，通过自我修改获得的经验可以提升智能体的下游能力和效率。

Original abstract: Advances in the coding capabilities of LLM agents allow them to inspect and modify their own instructions, tools, and execution procedures. Existing approaches use this ability to search for improved agents through repeated downstream evaluation, which incurs substantial costs and ties the search to the evaluated tasks. We introduce \textbf{SelfSearch}, a reward-free search procedure in which agents modify themselves using records of previous self-improvement episodes. These records capture the reasoning, tool actions, and outcomes of earlier modification attempts, providing concrete experience for improving both task solving and self-modification. Without downstream reward signals during search, SelfSearch improves population-mean success over the initial agent in all six model--benchmark settings, with individual agents gaining up to 11.2 percentage points on Terminal-Bench 2.1. On SWE-bench Multilingual, an agent improves success by \textbf{5.0} percentage points while reducing execution cost by \textbf{38.5}\% on tasks solved by both the initial and evolved agents. SelfSearch achieves competitive task success with evaluation-guided search baselines at lower search cost. With only \textbf{\$4.03} in search cost, it produces a harness that solves \textbf{82.0}\% of Terminal-Bench 2.1 tasks with DeepSeek V4 Flash under the settings of a public nine-harness comparison, matching the top-scoring harness, Codex. These results suggest that experience gained through self-modification can improve agents' downstream capabilities and efficiency.

</details>


### [93] [Retrieval-Augmented Skill Optimization via Cross-Harness Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38024)
*Jaewon Chu,Ji Soo Lee,Jihwan Park,Dohwan Ko,Jeehye Na,Seunghun Lee,Taehoon Lee,Minseo Yoon,Minseok Joo,Yunyang Xiong,Hyunwoo J. Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 16 pages

Selection reason: RASO用外部技能库检索增强agent技能优化，并做跨harness适配，与harness/技能层面的自动优化直接相关。

TL;DR: 提出RASO框架，利用外部技能库检索增强技能初始化与迭代更新，并通过跨执行框架适配迁移知识，在多个基准上持续优于无检索增强的基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能优化方法忽视了数百万公开共享技能中积累的知识，主要依赖昂贵的智能体rollout针对目标任务迭代精炼技能，且面临领域与执行框架不匹配的问题。

Method: RASO将外部技能语料库作为先验知识，检索相关技能知识并通过Cross-Harness Adaptation适配目标任务与执行框架；包含无需rollout的RASI初始化阶段和基于执行反馈检索外部知识进行迭代精炼的RASU更新阶段。

Result: 在四个智能体基准和两个模型上的大量实验表明，RASO持续优于未采用检索增强技能初始化与更新的基线。

Conclusion: 检索增强技能优化能够有效利用公开技能库中的积累知识，缓解对昂贵智能体rollout的依赖，并提升跨任务、跨执行框架的技能优化效果。

中文摘要：智能体技能是一种可复用、可操作的自然语言制品，用于指导智能体在给定执行框架（harness）下有效执行任务。近期研究探索了智能体技能的优化，推动了跨不同任务、领域和执行框架的公开可用技能集合不断增长。尽管已有数百万公开共享的技能，现有技能优化方法在很大程度上忽视了这些积累的知识，反而仅依赖昂贵的智能体 rollout 来针对目标任务迭代精炼技能。为解决这一问题，我们提出检索增强技能优化（Retrieval-Augmented Skill Optimization, RASO），一个在整个技能优化过程中利用外部技能语料库作为先验知识的框架。RASO 从现有技能中检索相关知识，并通过跨执行框架适配（Cross-Harness Adaptation）将其适配到目标任务和执行框架，以应对领域和执行框架两方面的不匹配。RASO 包含两个互补阶段：检索增强技能初始化（Retrieval-Augmented Skill Initialization, RASI）无需智能体 rollout 即可构建知识支撑的初始技能，而检索增强技能更新（Retrieval-Augmented Skill Update, RASU）通过执行反馈引导检索外部知识来迭代精炼技能。在四个智能体基准和两个模型上，大量实验表明，RASO 持续优于未采用检索增强技能初始化和更新的基线。

Original abstract: An agent skill is a reusable, actionable natural-language artifact that guides an agent to perform a task effectively under a given harness. Recent studies have explored the optimization of agent skills, contributing to a growing collection of publicly available skills spanning diverse tasks, domains, and harnesses. Despite millions of publicly shared skills, existing skill optimization methods largely overlook this accumulated knowledge, instead relying solely on expensive agent rollouts to iteratively refine skills for a target task. To address this, we propose \textbf{Retrieval-Augmented Skill Optimization (RASO)}, a framework that leverages an external skill corpus as prior knowledge throughout skill optimization. RASO retrieves relevant knowledge from existing skills and adapts it to the target task and harness via Cross-Harness Adaptation, accounting for mismatches in both domain and harness. RASO comprises two complementary stages: \textbf{Retrieval-Augmented Skill Initialization (RASI)} constructs a knowledge-grounded initial skill without requiring agent rollouts, while \textbf{Retrieval-Augmented Skill Update (RASU)} iteratively refines the skill by retrieving external knowledge guided by execution feedback. Across four agent benchmarks and two models, extensive experiments show that RASO consistently outperforms baselines without retrieval-augmented skill initialization and updating.

</details>


### [94] [UserProxyBench: Evaluating LLM User Simulators for Agent Benchmarks and Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38043)
*Ashish Jain,Armaan Sandhu*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 8 pages, 4 figures. Accepted to the Agentic AI Benchmarks and Applications for Enterprise Tasks Workshop (AABA4ET) at NeurIPS 2026

Selection reason: UserProxyBench评测agent基准中的LLM用户模拟器保真度，对agent评测与多轮交互可靠性有直接参考价值。

TL;DR: 提出 UserProxyBench 与 User Fidelity Score，直接评估用户模拟器保真度；发现用户代理变化显著影响任务奖励，过早披露是主要违规且会改变交互但不影响奖励，并给出成本-保真度前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交互式智能体基准和多轮强化学习常让第二个语言模型扮演用户，该模拟用户控制智能体获取信息的内容与时机，但基准通常只评估智能体，不直接衡量用户是否忠实执行了给定角色。

Method: 在 tau-bench 系列上构建 UserProxyBench 评估层，并提出 User Fidelity Score (UFS)，依据基准私有用户指令和任务锚定评分标准，独立于智能体成功与否进行评分；固定智能体为 GPT-5.5，仅变化用户代理，在 375 个企业任务和七个代理上评估。

Result: 仅改变用户代理即可使平均任务奖励变化 15.2 分；24.4% 的成功回合存在用户规范违规；主要失败模式是过早披露，即用户在信息被请求前提供信息；该行为对任务奖励影响很小，但在成功回合中使智能体平均少调用 1.06 次工具，改变了被评估的交互；并识别出经验性的成本-保真度前沿。

Conclusion: 用户模拟器的保真度应被直接评估，因为其行为会显著影响任务奖励并改变交互过程；成本-保真度前沿可帮助实践者选择满足所需保真度水平的最便宜模拟器。

中文摘要：交互式智能体基准和多轮强化学习越来越多地将第二个语言模型置于用户角色。这个模拟用户控制智能体接收什么信息以及何时接收，然而当前基准只对智能体评分，并不直接衡量用户是否正确执行了其指定角色。我们提出 UserProxyBench，一个建立在 tau-bench 系列之上的评估层，以及 User Fidelity Score (UFS)，它使用任务锚定的评分标准，独立于智能体成功与否，衡量对基准私有用户指令的遵循程度。将智能体固定为 GPT-5.5，仅改变用户代理，在 375 个企业任务上，平均任务奖励变化 15.2 分，同时 24.4% 的成功回合包含用户规范违规。主要失败是过早披露：用户在信息被请求之前就提供了信息。这种行为对任务奖励影响很小，但在成功回合中，它使智能体平均少进行 1.06 次工具调用，从而在保持奖励的同时改变了被评估的交互。最后，在七个代理中，我们识别出一个经验性的成本-保真度前沿，使实践者能够选择满足所需保真度水平的最便宜模拟器。

Original abstract: Interactive agent benchmarks and multi-turn reinforcement learning increasingly place a second language model in the role of the user. This simulated user controls what information the agent receives and when, yet current benchmarks score only the agent and do not directly measure whether the user correctly executed its assigned role. We introduce UserProxyBench, an evaluation layer over the tau-bench family, and the User Fidelity Score (UFS), which measures adherence to the benchmark's private user instructions using task-grounded rubric criteria scored independently of agent success. Holding the agent fixed at GPT-5.5 and varying only the user proxy across 375 enterprise tasks changes mean task reward by 15.2 points, while 24.4% of successful episodes contain a user-specification violation. The dominant failure is premature disclosure: users provide information before it is requested. This behavior has little effect on task reward, yet among successful episodes it causes the agent to make 1.06 fewer tool calls on average, changing the interaction being evaluated while preserving the reward. Finally, across seven proxies we identify an empirical cost-fidelity frontier, enabling practitioners to select the least expensive simulator that satisfies a required fidelity level.

</details>


### [95] [Character Training for Risk-Averse Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38093)
*Arav Dhoot,Punya Syon Pandey,Jamie Johnson,Daniel Tan,Elliott Thornley,David Demitri Africa*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 通过角色训练为agent注入风险厌恶偏好，属agent可靠性与安全控制的可迁移方法。

TL;DR: 通过性格训练与同策略蒸馏灌输CARA风险偏好，使未对齐AI更倾向风险规避；性格训练模型在未见基准格式下仍具竞争力，部分模型分布外泛化更好；token预算与模型选择影响最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 资源层面的风险厌恶可降低未对齐AI智能体造成灾难性伤害的可能性，因为风险规避的未对齐智能体会更偏好与人类交易等安全策略，而非反叛等高风险策略。

Method: 构建描述智能体资源上恒定绝对风险厌恶（CARA）的模型宪章，并通过同策略蒸馏进行灌输；采用性格训练来塑造风险偏好，并调节宪章的不同方面以分析其影响。

Result: 性格训练模型虽未见过基准测试决策格式，仍与直接在该格式上训练的基线具有竞争力，且在四个模型中的两个上分布外泛化更好；token预算和模型选择是灌输风险厌恶时最具影响力的因素。

Conclusion: 性格训练是一种有前景且可扩展的灌输广泛倾向的方法，可用于缓解未对齐AI智能体带来的风险。

中文摘要：对资源的风险厌恶可以防止未对齐的AI智能体造成灾难性伤害。未对齐但风险厌恶的智能体会倾向于选择更安全的策略，例如与人类达成交易，而不是更危险的策略，例如反叛。我们通过性格训练来训练智能体使其风险厌恶，发现人格特质为灌输风险偏好提供了一种稳健机制。为此，我们构建了一个模型宪章，描述智能体资源上的恒定绝对风险厌恶（CARA），并通过同策略蒸馏将其灌输给模型。尽管在训练期间从未见过基准测试的决策格式，性格训练模型仍与直接在该格式上训练的基线模型具有竞争力，并且在我们四个模型中的两个上，其分布外泛化能力优于基线。我们还调节了宪章的不同方面，发现token预算和模型选择是性格训练中灌输风险厌恶最具影响力的方面。我们从这些结果得出结论：性格训练是一种有前景且可扩展的方式，用于灌输广泛的倾向，我们可以利用它来降低未对齐AI智能体带来的风险。

Original abstract: Risk aversion in resources could prevent misaligned AI agents from causing catastrophic harm. Misaligned but risk-averse agents would tend to favor safer strategies like making deals with humans over riskier strategies like rebelling. We train agents to be risk averse through character training, finding that persona traits provide a robust mechanism for instilling risk preferences. To do this, we construct a model constitution describing constant absolute risk aversion (CARA) over an agent's resources and instill it through on-policy distillation. Despite never seeing the benchmark's decision format during training, character-trained models are competitive with baselines trained directly on it, and generalise better than them out of distribution on two of our four models. We also modulate different aspects of the constitution, finding that token budget and model choice are the most influential aspect of character training to instill risk aversion. We conclude from these results that character training is a promising and scalable way to instil broad dispositions, which we can use to our advantage in mitigating risk from misaligned AI agents.

</details>


### [96] [Do LLM Agents Execute the Plans They Declare? From Planning-Mode Declaration to Pattern-Specific Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38108)
*Subba Reddy Oota,Francisco Herrera,Jordi Cabot Sagrera,Marcos López de Prado,Shadab Khan*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 51 pages, 8 figures

Selection reason: 提出Planning-as-Routing研究规划声明与执行的一致性，路由到模式专用执行器并在SWE-bench验证，对coding agent规划执行有重要启发。

TL;DR: 论文提出 Planning-as-Routing 框架，将规划模式选择与执行分离，发现模式专用执行器显著缩小执行差距，但 LLM 仍难以可靠选择最佳规划模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有规划器—执行器系统失败时，仅凭最终任务成功无法区分是计划选择错误还是执行不忠实，因此需要研究计划声明—执行差距，并分别评估模式选择与忠实执行。

Method: 让 LLM 声明四种规划模式（Predefined、Sequential、Hierarchical、Search）之一，由确定性路由器分派给相应模式专用执行器；在四个基准和三个 LLM 上评估，并与通用 Plan+ReAct 比较。

Result: 三个一致发现：通用 Plan+ReAct 常不保持声明结构，仅 22%–45% 轨迹保持；模式有效性随环境/模型变化，Search 在 ALFWorld、Hierarchical 在 SWE-bench 最好；执行带来最大增益，ALFWorld 成功率 0.48→0.92，SWE-bench Verified 0.36→0.44；少样本示例在某些组合改善模式选择，但当前 LLM 仍不可靠。

Conclusion: 可靠智能体规划需同时具备有效模式选择与忠实执行；路由显著缩小执行差距，任务特定模式选择仍是开放问题。

中文摘要：大型语言模型（LLMs）使智能体能够通过生成计划并在环境中执行该计划来解决长时程任务。然而，成功的规划需要两种不同能力：为任务选择适当的计划，并忠实地执行它。现有的规划器—执行器系统可能在这两个阶段中的任一阶段失败，而仅靠最终任务成功无法区分选择失败与执行失败。因此，我们研究计划声明—执行差距（Plan Declaration–Execution Gap），并引入 Planning-as-Routing：LLM 声明四种规划模式之一：预定义（Predefined）、顺序（Sequential）、分层（Hierarchical）或搜索（Search），然后由确定性路由器将任务分派给相应的模式专用执行器。在四个基准和三个 LLM 上，我们发现三个一致的模式。第一，通用的 Plan+ReAct 往往无法保持所声明的规划结构，尤其是在较长计划中：在三个基准上，仅有 22%–45% 的轨迹保持该结构，而模式专用执行器会强制执行预期结构。第二，规划模式的有效性因环境和模型而异：Search 在 ALFWorld 上表现最佳，Hierarchical 在 SWE-bench 上表现最佳，并且在同一基准内，最强模式可能因模型而异。第三，最大的增益来自执行：相较于 Plan+ReAct，模式专用执行器将 ALFWorld 上的任务成功率从（0.48）提升到（0.92），并将 SWE-bench Verified 上的成功率从（0.36）提升到（0.44）。然而，当前 LLM 并不能可靠地为每个任务选择最强模式，尽管少样本示例在某些基准—模型组合中改善了选择。总体而言，可靠的智能体规划既需要有效的模式选择，也需要忠实的执行：路由显著缩小了执行差距，而任务特定的模式选择仍然有待解决。

Original abstract: Large language models (LLMs) enable agents to solve long-horizon tasks by generating a plan and then executing it in an environment. However, successful planning requires two distinct capabilities: selecting an appropriate plan for the task and executing it faithfully. Existing planner--executor systems can fail at either stage, while final task success alone cannot distinguish selection from execution failures. We therefore study the Plan Declaration--Execution Gap and introduce Planning-as-Routing, where an LLM declares one of four planning modes: Predefined, Sequential, Hierarchical, or Search, and a deterministic router dispatches the task to the corresponding pattern-specific executor. Across four benchmarks and three LLMs, we find three consistent patterns. First, generic Plan+ReAct often fails to preserve declared planning structure, especially for longer plans: across three benchmarks, only (22)--(45%) of trajectories preserve it, whereas pattern-specific executors enforce the intended structure. Second, planning-mode effectiveness varies across environments and models: Search performs best on ALFWorld, Hierarchical on SWE-bench, and the strongest pattern can vary across models within the same benchmark. Third, the largest gains come from execution: pattern-specific executors improve task success from (0.48) to (0.92) on ALFWorld and from (0.36) to (0.44) on SWE-bench Verified over Plan+ReAct. Current LLMs, however, do not reliably select the strongest mode for each task, although few-shot examples improve selection in some benchmark--model combinations. Overall, reliable agent planning requires both effective mode selection and faithful execution: routing substantially closes the execution gap, while task-specific mode selection remains open.

</details>


### [97] [AdviSD: Learning to Advise Frontier LLMs via Targeted Multi-Turn Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38142)
*Rishabh Agrawal,Hejie Cui,Shasha Li,Shanchan Wu,Sercan Ö. Arık*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: AdviSD训练小型advisor通过反思自蒸馏指导冻结的LLM执行器，涉及选择式监督与跨模型迁移，可迁移到agent的反馈/反思机制。

TL;DR: AdviSD 通过选择性自蒸馏利用反馈纠正来训练顾问，避免低价值纠正损害学习，并在多个基准和迁移设置上优于 GRPO 与随机选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 顾问可从交互反馈中改进建议，但看似合理却不改变执行的纠正仍会影响未来决策；在共享参数模型中，若这些纠正的目标对有用建议的偏好更弱，学习它们可能限制性能。

Method: AdviSD 选择性地将基于结果的强化学习与来自一个以反馈为条件的顾问副本的自蒸馏结合；反思生成纠正，顾问对同一执行器响应在有无建议时打分，并按差异幅度选择监督决策，无需执行器似然或额外 rollout。

Result: 面向 Gemini 和 Claude 的 Qwen3-8B 顾问实验中，AdviSD 在 BFCL-v3 上比 advisor-GRPO 高 4.2-6.4 个百分点，在 EnvScaler 上高 3.9-5.1 分；可泛化到域外任务并跨执行器版本和模型家族迁移，且优于匹配数量的随机选择。

Conclusion: 有选择地从反馈条件自蒸馏中学习纠正，可提升顾问的最终性能、泛化与迁移能力，其选择规则是关键。

中文摘要：一个小型可训练顾问可以使用自然语言建议来引导一个冻结的语言模型执行器。除了从任务奖励中学习外，顾问还可以利用已完成交互的反馈来改进其建议。然而，一个看似合理的纠正不一定改变执行，但从此类纠正中学习仍可能影响顾问在其他情境中的未来决策。在共享参数模型中，我们证明，如果这些纠正的目标相较其他纠正的目标对有用建议的偏好更弱，那么此类纠正可能会限制学习。与从每个纠正中学习相比，比其他纠正更少地保留这些纠正可改善模型的最终性能。受此启发，我们的方法——顾问自蒸馏（AdviSD）——选择性地将基于结果的强化学习与来自一个以反馈为条件的顾问副本的自蒸馏相结合。反思提出纠正，顾问在有无其给出的建议两种条件下对同一记录的执行器响应进行评分，并利用差异幅度选择用于监督的决策。该方法不需要执行器似然或额外的执行器 rollout。使用面向 Gemini 和 Claude 的 Qwen3-8B 顾问进行的实验表明，AdviSD 在 BFCL-v3 上比 advisor-GRPO 高出 4.2-6.4 个百分点，在 EnvScaler 上高出 3.9-5.1 分。训练后的顾问可泛化到域外任务，并跨不同执行器版本和模型家族迁移。AdviSD 也优于匹配数量的随机选择，支持其选择规则的价值。

Original abstract: A small trainable advisor can steer a frozen language-model executor using natural-language advice. In addition to learning from task rewards, the advisor can use feedback from completed interactions to improve its advice. However, a plausible correction need not change execution, yet learning from such corrections can still affect the advisor's future decisions in other contexts. In a shared-parameter model, we prove that such corrections can limit learning if their targets favor useful advice less strongly than those of other corrections. Keeping them less often than the rest improves the model's eventual performance compared to learning from every correction. Motivated by this, our method, Advisor Self-Distillation (AdviSD), pairs outcome-based reinforcement learning with self-distillation from a feedback-conditioned copy of the advisor selectively. Reflection proposes corrections, and the advisor scores the same recorded executor response with and without its issued advice, using the magnitude of the difference to select decisions for supervision. This approach does not require executor likelihoods or additional executor rollouts. Experiments with Qwen3-8B advisors for Gemini and Claude show that AdviSD outperforms advisor-GRPO by 4.2-6.4 percentage points on BFCL-v3 and by 3.9-5.1 score points on EnvScaler. The trained advisors generalize to out-of-domain tasks and transfer across different executor versions and model families. AdviSD also beats matched-count random selection, supporting the value of its selection rule.

</details>


### [98] [Learning Meta-Skills for Agent Harness Design in Test-Time AI4AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38143)
*Cheng Qian,Kunlun Zhu,Beibin Li,Zhenhailong Wang,Heng Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 22 Pages, 4 Figures, 5 Tables

Selection reason: 研究agent harness设计与可复用meta-skill经验库，直接涉及agent环境构建、经验复用与系统级自我改进，对SemOpt的harness/workflow设计有明确迁移价值。

TL;DR: 论文研究测试时 AI-for-AI：在权重固定下，Builder 学习为 Target 构建更优执行环境；通过 Meta-Skill 从执行反馈中学习可复用原则，并在 Harness-Bench 与 NewtonBench 上显著提升性能，展示系统级自我改进路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体性能受推理能力与行动环境共同影响。本文关注测试阶段如何在不更新模型权重的情况下，让 Builder 学会为 Target 构造更好的执行环境，并将经验转化为可复用的支持，以探索系统级自我改进。

Method: 提出 Meta-Skill，即规定何时需要支持以及应提供何种资源的原则。Builder 从 Target 在开发集上的执行反馈中学习这些原则，形成冻结的技能库，再利用该技能库为未见任务构建 harness。

Result: 在 Harness-Bench 和 NewtonBench 上，完整技能库的 meta-skill 相比无技能构建将宏平均性能提升 8.95 个百分点，相比将同一技能库直接交付给 Target 提升 12.02 个百分点；当同一模型兼任两个角色时也获得增益。

Conclusion: 将经验转化为可执行支持具有重要价值；学会构建更好环境可能是实现系统级自我改进的一条路径。

中文摘要：智能体的性能既取决于推理能力，也取决于其行动所处的环境。我们研究测试时的 AI-for-AI，即探究在 Builder 和 Target 两个模型的权重均保持不变的情况下，Builder 如何学习为 Target 构建更好的执行环境。为使 Builder 的经验可复用，我们引入 Meta-Skill：规定何时需要支持以及应提供何种资源的原则。Builder 在开发集上从 Target 的执行反馈中学习这些原则，然后使用冻结的技能库为未见任务构建 harness。在 Harness-Bench 和 NewtonBench 上，完整技能库的 meta-skill 相比无技能构建将宏平均性能提高 8.95 个百分点，相比将同一技能库直接交付给 Target 提高 12.02 个百分点。这些结果凸显了将经验转化为可执行支持的价值。当同一模型同时充当两个角色时也取得增益，这进一步表明了一条通过学会构建更好环境来实现系统级自我改进的路径。

Original abstract: Agent performance depends on both reasoning ability and the environment in which it acts. We study test-time AI-for-AI, asking how a Builder can learn to construct better execution environments for a Target while both models' weights remain fixed. To make the Builder's experience reusable, we introduce Meta-Skill: principles specifying when support is needed and what resources to provide. The Builder learns these principles from Target's execution feedback on the development set, then uses the frozen skill bank to construct harnesses for unseen tasks. Across Harness-Bench and NewtonBench, full-bank meta-skills improve macro-average performance by 8.95 percentage points over no-skill construction, and 12.02 points over direct delivery of the same bank to the Target. These results highlight the value of translating experience into executable support. Gains when the same model serves both roles further suggest a path to system level self-improvement through learning to build better environments.

</details>


### [99] [Thinking Before Thinking: Scaling Agentic Inference Through Meta-Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38147)
*Paras Dahal,Anton Bakhtin,Taco Cohen,Zhengxing Chen,Carole-Jean Wu,Rob Fergus,Scott Yih,Gabriel Synnaeve,Ruslan Salakhutdinov,Sanjeev Arora,Jason Weston,Anirudh Goyal*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 提出agentic meta-reasoning控制框架，含规划、持久记忆、长程执行与预算分配，并在ProgramBench等程序重建任务上验证，对coding agent长程控制研究高度相关。

TL;DR: 提出智能体元推理：在推理时用控制器显式管理长时程智能体执行，通过整合状态、探索选项、预算评估和记忆分派提升长程任务表现；在多个基准上优于直接控制及生产级基线，但小预算下开销可能有害。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体处理更长、更复杂任务时，执行控制本身成为关键问题；每一步都有继续、重启或停止等选择，需要显式且结构化地做控制决策。

Method: 将任务级计算交给 workers，控制器整合运行状态、探索后续选项、在剩余预算下评估选项价值，并从持久记忆提供上下文分派工作；决策间只保留运行的精简记录而非完整历史。基线包括生产级编码智能体、研究框架，以及使用相同 workers 和计算预算的直接控制智能体。

Result: ProgramBench 上，GPT-5.5 下达到 71.5%，而 Codex 为 58.0%；Opus 4.8 下达到 67.2%，而 Claude Code 为 65.5%。其他基准涵盖抽象推理、多领域长时程推理和证明生成，在三个前沿模型上平均比直接控制提升 3.6 到 4.2 个百分点；在直接控制平台期的预算范围内持续提升，小预算下开销可能有害。工件图分析显示更多复用早期工作、多数设置中更高正确解覆盖率，以及最终选择中的非均匀收益。

Conclusion: 结果表明，随着智能体扩展到更长运行，将计算投入结构化控制变得更加重要。

中文摘要：随着智能体承担更长、更复杂的问题，控制执行本身成为一项独立任务。运行中的每一步都会带来新的控制选择，例如基于哪部分中间工作继续构建、是否重新开始，或何时停止。我们提出智能体元推理（agentic meta-reasoning），一种推理时框架，使这些选择成为显式且结构化的推理过程。工作者执行任务级计算，而控制器整合运行中已确立的内容，探索下一步选项，评估每个选项在剩余预算下的价值，并根据来自持久记忆的上下文分派所选工作。在决策之间，控制器只携带运行的紧凑记录，而不是重放其完整历史。我们的基线涵盖生产级编码智能体和研究框架，以及一个使用相同工作者和计算预算额度的直接控制智能体。在通过程序重建测试长时程智能体能力的 ProgramBench 上，元推理在使用 GPT-5.5 时达到 71.5%，而 Codex 为 58.0%；在使用 Opus 4.8 时达到 67.2%，而 Claude Code 为 65.5%。在其他基准上，涵盖抽象推理、多领域长时程推理和证明生成，它在三个前沿模型上平均比直接控制提升 3.6 到 4.2 个百分点。在直接控制趋于平台期的测试预算范围内，它持续改进，尽管其开销在预算较小时可能造成损害。工件图分析显示，对早期工作的复用更多，在大多数设置中正确解的覆盖率更高，且最终选择中的收益不均匀。这些结果表明，随着智能体扩展到更长的运行，将计算花费在结构化控制上变得更加重要。

Original abstract: As agents take on longer and more complex problems, controlling the execution becomes a task in its own right. Each step in the run brings new control choices, like which partial work to build on, whether to start fresh, or when to stop. We introduce agentic meta-reasoning, an inference-time harness that makes these choices an explicit and structured reasoning process. Workers carry out the task-level computation, while a controller consolidates what the run has established, explores next options, assesses what each option is worth under the remaining budget, and dispatches the chosen work with context drawn from persistent memory. Between decisions the controller carries only a compact account of the run rather than replaying its full history. Our baselines span production coding agents and research harnesses, together with a Direct Control Agent using the same workers and compute budget allowance. On ProgramBench, which tests long-horizon agentic capability through program reconstruction, meta-reasoning achieves 71.5% with GPT-5.5 against 58.0% for Codex; with Opus 4.8 it achieves 67.2% against 65.5% for Claude Code. On the other benchmarks, spanning abstract reasoning, multi-domain long-horizon reasoning, and proof generation, it gains between 3.6 and 4.2 points over direct control, averaged across three frontier models. It keeps improving over the tested budget ranges where direct control plateaus, though its overhead can hurt at small budgets. Artifact-graph analysis reveals more reuse of earlier work, higher coverage of correct solutions in most settings, and nonuniform gains in final selection. These results indicate that spending computation on structured control becomes more important as agents scale to longer runs.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [100] [Agent-Callable Feature Coverage: Measuring Software Readiness for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35789)
*Zedong Peng*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: 提出 agent 可调用能力覆盖度(ACFC/ARC)指标与实验，为 coding/computer-use agent 的工具接口可发现性与可控性评估提供可迁移度量。

TL;DR: 论文提出GUI-API对等原则，并给出ACFC与ARC指标来评估软件对AI代理的结构化支持；对30个系统及10个部署系统的实验显示，可发现性与安全治理是主要短板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI代理已能通过截图操作图形软件，关键问题转为软件是否提供结构化、可调用且安全可控的接口；现有二元API覆盖率不足以刻画代理就绪度。

Method: 将需求形式化为GUI-API对等原则；提出ACFC产品级覆盖率指标和ARC框架，ARC按可访问性、可发现性、可控性各0-3分、总分0-9评估每项能力；借鉴Richardson成熟度模型；对5类30个软件系统进行实证评估，并在10个已部署系统的受控测试平台中用三种代理模型测试，含文档消融和不可访问能力负对照。

Result: 基于ARC的ACFC分数低于仅看可访问性的预期：多数系统API覆盖中等，但面向代理的文档与安全治理滞后。测试平台中，仅API代理完成58项可访问任务中的56项；文档消融降至50/58；不可访问负对照未解决；结果在三种代理模型上成立，包括本地运行的Google开放权重Gemma4。

Conclusion: 软件需从单纯API暴露转向面向代理的完整就绪：同时提升可访问性、可发现性和可控性，并补足文档与安全治理；ARC/ACFC可作为诊断与改进工具。

中文摘要：AI代理已经通过基于截图的计算机使用来操作图形软件，因此紧迫的问题不是代理能否操作软件，而是软件通过结构化、可控渠道对它们的支持有多好。我们将这一需求形式化为GUI-API对等原则：人类用户通过图形界面可用的每一项能力，也应通过具有适当安全元数据的结构化、可调用接口供代理访问。为落实这一原则，我们提出两项贡献。第一，代理可调用功能覆盖率（Agent-Callable Feature Coverage, ACFC），一种产品级就绪度指标，量化代理能够访问和使用系统面向人类功能的程度。第二，代理就绪一致性（Agent Readiness Conformance, ARC），一个从三个轴对每项能力进行评分的框架：可访问性（代理能否调用它）、可发现性（代理能否找到并理解它）和可控性（代理能否安全地使用它），每项采用0-3分，得出0-9的复合就绪度分数。借鉴Richardson成熟度模型，ARC提供了比二元覆盖率更细粒度的评估。在一项涵盖五个类别、30个软件系统的实证研究中，基于ARC的ACFC分数显著低于仅可访问性指标所暗示的水平：大多数系统实现了中等程度的API覆盖，但在面向代理的文档和安全治理方面落后。在由十个已部署系统组成的受控测试平台中，仅使用API的代理完成了58项任务中的56项，这些任务的目标能力通过结构化接口暴露；一项文档消融实验将其降至58项中的50项，表明可发现性即使在可访问能力中也会影响执行，而作为负对照纳入的不可访问能力则未被解决。这些发现适用于三种代理模型，包括在本地运行的Google开放权重模型Gemma4。

Original abstract: AI agents already operate graphical software through screenshot-based computer use, so the pressing question is not whether agents can operate software, but how well software supports them through structured, controllable channels. We formalize this need as the GUI-API parity principle: every capability available to human users through a graphical interface should also be accessible to agents through a structured, callable interface with appropriate safety metadata. To operationalize this principle, we introduce two contributions. First, Agent-Callable Feature Coverage (ACFC), a product-level readiness metric quantifying how much of a system's human-facing functionality agents can access and use. Second, Agent Readiness Conformance (ARC), a framework that scores each capability on three axes: Accessibility (can an agent call it), Discoverability (can an agent find and understand it), and Controllability (can an agent use it safely), each on a 0-3 scale, yielding a composite 0-9 readiness score. Drawing on the Richardson Maturity Model, ARC provides finer-grained assessment than binary coverage. In an empirical study of 30 software systems across five categories, ARC-based ACFC scores are substantially lower than Accessibility-only metrics would suggest: most systems achieve moderate API coverage but lag on agent-oriented documentation and safety governance. In a controlled testbed of ten deployed systems, API-only agents completed 56 of 58 tasks whose target capabilities were exposed through structured interfaces; a documentation ablation reduced this to 50 of 58, showing that Discoverability affects execution even among accessible capabilities, while inaccessible capabilities, included as negative controls, were not solved. These findings hold across three agent models, including Google's open-weights Gemma4 run locally.

</details>


### [101] [A Leakage-Safe, Cost-Aware Regression Testing Methodology for the Quantum Transpiler](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35834)
*Furqan Nasir,Muhammad Arif Shah,Ifitkhar Alam*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: 25 pages, 4 figures

Selection reason: 量子转译器的预算受限回归测试选择与优先级方法，含泄漏安全、成本感知的预注册评测，可迁移到 CI 测试选择与基准验证方法。

TL;DR: 该研究为 Qiskit 量子转译器提出防泄漏、成本感知的回归测试选择方法；预注册评估显示透明风险评分复合选择器未优于基线，消融表明多样性与新颖性才是有效信号，随后多样性优先选择器匹配最强基线并优于原复合方法，同时发布全部工件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子 SDK 持续修订，单次转译器 pass 变更可能引入回归；CI 需在固定预算下从大规模、成本异质测试套件中选择并优先执行测试，同时避免评估中的数据泄漏，并诚实检验选择器的真实有效性。

Method: 提出面向 Qiskit 量子转译器的防泄漏、成本感知回归测试选择方法；采用预注册、预算绑定评估，在读取测试预言标签前依据实测成本固定预算；使用 116 单元、成本差距 9945 倍、T_full=69.17s 的成本异质语料库；比较 risk_score 选择器与多样性、成本/历史/变更阶段、随机等基线；进行组件消融；开展第二个独立预注册评估，含 19 个变异测试事件（9 原始+10 新验证算子），检验分解式多样性优先选择器；并用两个真实 Qiskit CI 前向回归事件佐证。

Result: risk_score 选择器平均检测-预算 AUC 0.721 [95% CI 0.44,0.94]，低于仅多样性 0.874 [0.65,1.00] (Cliff's δ=-0.64，大效应)、成本/历史/变更阶段 0.840 和随机 0.821；消融显示复合选择器不及其最佳信号，多样性和新颖性而非严重性或成本为有效信号。第二个评估中，多样性优先选择器与最强基线持平（0.880 vs 0.880, δ=-0.08），但显著优于原复合选择器（0.880 vs 0.768, δ=+0.57）。两个真实 Qiskit CI 前向回归事件按事件报告并佐证。

Conclusion: 阴性结果按预注册方式诚实报告并解释机制：在该场景中，多样性与新颖性信号优于风险评分及成本等复合信号；分解式多样性优先选择器更有效。全部代码、数据和工件已发布以支持可复现性。

中文摘要：诸如 Qiskit 之类的量子 SDK 会持续修订，而单次转译器 pass 的更改就可能引入软件回归，因此持续集成（CI）必须在固定预算下从大型、成本异质的测试套件中选择并优先排序测试。我们提出了一种面向 Qiskit 量子转译器的、防泄漏且成本感知的回归测试选择方法，并采用预注册、预算约束的评估以避免数据泄漏。该评估使用一个成本异质语料库（116 个单元，每单元成本差距为 9,945 倍，T_full = 69.17 秒），其预算是在读取任何测试预言标签之前，根据实测成本固定下来的。一个透明的 risk_score 选择器未能击败简单的测试用例优先级排序基线：平均检测-预算 AUC 为 0.721 [95% CI 0.44, 0.94]，而仅多样性方法为 0.874 [0.65, 1.00]（Cliff's δ = -0.64，大效应），成本/历史/变更阶段基线为 0.840，随机方法为 0.821。组件消融表明，该复合选择器表现不及它自身的最佳信号：有效信号是多样性和新颖性，而不是严重性或成本。第二个独立预注册的评估（19 个变异测试事件：九个原始事件加十个新的、已验证的变异算子）检验了该机制所暗示的分解式、多样性优先选择器：它将与最强基线的差距缩小到可忽略的效应量（0.880 对 0.880，δ = -0.08），同时决定性地击败原始复合选择器（0.880 对 0.768，δ = +0.57）。来自真实 Qiskit CI 历史的两个已验证前向回归事件佐证了这一点，并在预先声明的声明范围规则下按事件而非合并报告。我们把这个阴性结果作为一个诚实的、预注册的发现及其机制来报告。本实证软件工程研究的所有代码、数据和工件均已发布，以支持可复现性。

Original abstract: Quantum SDKs such as Qiskit are revised continually, and a single transpiler-pass change can introduce a software regression, so continuous integration (CI) must select and prioritize tests from a large, cost-heterogeneous suite under a fixed budget. We present a leakage-safe, cost-aware regression-test-selection methodology for the Qiskit quantum transpiler, with a pre-registered, budget binding evaluation that avoids data leakage. The evaluation uses a cost-heterogeneous corpus (116 units, per-unit cost spread 9,945X, T_full = 69.17 s) whose budgets were fixed from measured cost before any test-oracle label was read. A transparent risk_score selector does not beat simple test case-prioritization baselines: mean detection-vs-budget AUC is 0.721 [95% CI 0.44, 0.94] versus 0.874 [0.65, 1.00] for diversity-only (Cliff's δ = -0.64, large), with cost/history/change-stage baselines at 0.840 and random at 0.821. A component ablation shows the composite underperforms its own best signal: diversity and novelty, not severity or cost, are the effective signal. A second, independently pre-registered evaluation (19 mutation-testing events: nine original plus ten new, verified operators) tests the decomposed, diversity-first selector this mechanism implies: it closes the gap to the strongest baseline to a negligible effect size (0.880 vs 0.880, δ = -0.08) while decisively beating the original composite (0.880 vs 0.768, δ = +0.57). Two verified forward-regression events from real Qiskit CI history corroborate it, reported per event, not pooled, under a pre-declared claim-scope rule. We report the negative result as an honest, pre-registered finding with its mechanism. All code, data, and artifacts from this empirical software engineering study are released for reproducibility.

</details>


### [102] [Beyond Rule-Based Mutation Testing: Test-Aware Mutant Generation Using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35841)
*Nils Kiele,Zainab Saad,Zirui Wang,Steve Drew,Samira Ebrahimi Kahou*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: 20 pages, 7 figures. Accepted to ESEM 2026

DOI: [10.4230/LIPIcs.ESEM.2026.49](https://doi.org/10.4230/LIPIcs.ESEM.2026.49)

Selection reason: 用 LLM 做测试感知的变异体生成，显著提升有效故障率并降低单故障成本，直接服务于 agent 测试生成与测试套件评估。

TL;DR: 提出测试感知的 LLM 变异体生成方法，让模型在提示中看到问题陈述、规范解和基础测试，从而生成能通过基础测试但可被扩展测试捕获的真实缺陷；在 HumanEval 和 MBPP 上，其已验证故障率显著高于测试盲提示和传统工具 mutmut。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于规则的变异测试工具会生成大量琐碎、冗余或等价变异体，难以有效识别测试套件缺口；现有 LLM 方法虽能生成更真实故障，但多为测试盲，无法利用已有单元测试信息。

Method: 提出测试感知变异体生成：在单个提示中向 LLM 提供问题陈述、规范解和基础测试，要求生成通过基础单元测试的非平凡变异体。在 HumanEval 和 MBPP 上评估 Gemini 3.1 Pro、Gemini 3 Flash、GPT 5.1 Codex Mini、GPT 4.1 Mini、Qwen3-32B 五个模型，并用扩展 EvalPlus 测试套件作为自动预言机验证存活变异体是否为真实缺陷。

Result: 测试感知提示的已验证故障率为 HumanEval 87.7%、MBPP 79.1%，远高于匹配的测试盲提示（12.2%、23.0%）和传统规则工具 mutmut（4.4%、5.7%）。三种方法的故障细微程度相当，但测试感知方法相比测试盲提示降低了每个已验证故障的计算成本。

Conclusion: 将现有单元测试暴露给 LLM，可使变异体生成从无针对性缺陷注入转向有效发现现有测试套件弱点，并为未来扩展到生产级测试感知变异体生成奠定基础。

中文摘要：变异测试通过向程序代码注入合成故障来评估测试套件充分性。然而，传统基于规则的工具往往生成大量琐碎、冗余或等价变异体，限制了其在识别测试套件缺口方面的实际应用。尽管近期基于大语言模型（LLM）的方法能生成更真实的故障，但大多数仍是测试盲的：模型只看到源代码，无法推理现有测试已经覆盖了什么。我们提出测试感知的变异体生成：LLM在单个提示中接收问题陈述、规范解和基础测试，并且必须生成一个能通过基础单元测试的非平凡变异体。我们在 HumanEval 和 MBPP 基准上，跨五个 LLM——Gemini 3.1 Pro、Gemini 3 Flash、GPT 5.1 Codex Mini、GPT 4.1 Mini、Qwen3-32B——评估了该方法。扩展的 EvalPlus 测试套件作为自动预言机，用于验证存活变异体是否代表真实缺陷。测试感知提示得到的已验证故障率分别为 87.7%（HumanEval）和 79.1%（MBPP），意味着这些变异体通过所有基础测试，但会被预言机捕获。这远远优于匹配的测试盲提示（分别仅得到 12.2% 和 23.0%）以及传统基于规则的工具 mutmut（4.4% 和 5.7%）。尽管故障细微程度（变异体未通过扩展测试的比例）在三种方法之间仍相当，但与测试盲提示相比，测试感知将每个已验证故障的计算成本降至最低。让 LLM 接触现有单元测试，使变异体生成从无针对性的缺陷注入转向有效发现现有测试套件中的弱点。我们的工作为未来研究将测试感知变异体生成扩展到生产级环境奠定了具体基础。

Original abstract: Mutation testing evaluates test-suite adequacy by injecting synthetic faults into program code. However, traditional rule-based tools often generate large numbers of trivial, redundant, or equivalent mutants that limit their practical use for identifying gaps in a test suite. While recent large language model (LLM)-based approaches generate more realistic faults, most remain test-blind: The model sees only the source code and cannot reason about what existing tests already cover. We propose test-aware mutant generation, in which an LLM receives the problem statement, canonical solution and base tests in a single prompt, and must generate a nontrivial mutant that passes the base unit tests. We evaluate this approach across a set of five LLMs -- Gemini 3.1 Pro, Gemini 3 Flash, GPT 5.1 Codex Mini, GPT 4.1 Mini, Qwen3-32B -- on the HumanEval and MBPP benchmarks. The extended EvalPlus test suites serve as an automated oracle to verify whether surviving mutants represent genuine bugs. Test-aware prompting yields verified fault rates of 87.7% (HumanEval) and 79.1% (MBPP), meaning these mutants pass all base tests but are caught by the oracle. This vastly outperforms the matched test-blind prompting (which yields only 12.2% and 23.0%, respectively) and the traditional rule-based tool mutmut (4.4% and 5.7%). While fault subtlety (the fraction of extended tests a mutant fails) remains comparable across all three methods, test-awareness minimizes the computational cost per verified fault, compared to test-blind prompting. Exposing an LLM to existing unit tests shifts mutant generation from untargeted bug injection toward effective discovery of weaknesses in an existing test suite. Our work establishes a concrete foundation for future research to scale test-aware mutant generation to production-level environments.

</details>


### [103] [UNBIND: UNlearning By INference-time Directional Steering for Code LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35913)
*Zhengyang Shan,Jiayun Xin,Yanjun Lin,Xu Qian,Zhiang Liu,Minghui Xu,Yue Zhang,Qin Hu,Kun Li,Xiuzhen Cheng*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: 推理期方向性引导的代码遗忘框架，在保持权重不变下抑制目标代码复现，可迁移到代码 LLM 行为控制与可靠性干预。

TL;DR: UNBIND 在固定模型权重下，通过分离目标代码对应的隐藏状态方向并在推理时抑制其复现，实现选择性代码遗忘，在多种设置中兼顾遗忘效果与编程能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码模型可能记住需移除的实现，但目标代码与保留代码共享计算模式，导致遗忘特定实现与保留通用编程能力之间存在张力。

Method: 提出 UNBIND 代码遗忘框架，分别识别目标代码对应的隐藏状态并构建抑制复现的独立方向，在推理阶段进行选择性遗忘，同时保持模型权重不变。

Result: 在 2 个代码模型、2 个语料和 14 个基线上，UNBIND 均取得最高联合遗忘与效用得分；F-BLEU 目标复现降低 97.3%--99.1%，HumanEval+ 最多少 2 题、MBPP+ 最多少 6 题；固定预算重复抽取中，至少 50 token 精确跨度的目标从 188--262/300 降至 0--2/300，无跨度达 100 token，平均最佳恢复率为 0.43%--6.45%。

Conclusion: 多语言及相关代码评估显示 UNBIND 能有效遗忘且对有用编程能力影响有限，是一种实用的选择性代码遗忘方法。

中文摘要：代码大语言模型从大型代码语料中习得编程能力，但也可能记住此后需要移除的实现。当出现版权或安全担忧时，需要代码遗忘来控制其继续复现。然而，目标代码与保留代码共享计算模式，这在遗忘特定实现与保留通用编程能力之间造成张力。我们提出 UNBIND，一种代码遗忘框架，它分别考虑哪些隐藏状态对应于目标代码，以及如何抑制其复现。通过为这些目标构建独立方向，UNBIND 在推理时实现选择性遗忘，同时保持模型权重固定。我们的评估涵盖两个代码模型和两个语料上的十四个基线。UNBIND 在每种设置下都取得最高的联合遗忘与效用得分。以 F-BLEU 衡量，它将目标代码复现降低 97.3% 至 99.1%，与原始模型相比，最多少解决两个 HumanEval+ 问题和六个 MBPP+ 问题。在固定预算下的重复抽取测试中，每个设置 300 个目标里，产生至少 50 个 token 的精确跨度的目标数量从 188--262 降至 0--2。没有抽取跨度达到 100 个 token，平均最佳恢复率范围为 0.43% 至 6.45%。多语言和相关代码评估进一步表明，在有效遗忘的同时对有用编程能力影响有限，支持 UNBIND 作为选择性代码遗忘的实用方法。

Original abstract: Code large language models acquire programming capabilities from large code corpora, but can also memorize implementations that later require removal. Code unlearning is needed to control their continued reproduction when copyright or security concerns arise. However, targeted and retained code share computational patterns, creating a tension between forgetting specific implementations and preserving general programming ability. We propose \textbf{UNBIND}, a code unlearning framework that separately considers which hidden states correspond to the target code and how to suppress its reproduction. By constructing separate directions for these objectives, UNBIND achieves selective unlearning at inference time while keeping model weights fixed. Our evaluation covers fourteen baselines across two code models and two corpora. UNBIND achieves the highest joint forgetting and utility score in every setting. It reduces target code reproduction by 97.3\% to 99.1\% as measured by F-BLEU, with at most two fewer HumanEval+ and six fewer MBPP+ problems solved than the original models. In repeated extraction tests under a fixed budget, the number of targets yielding exact spans of at least 50 tokens falls from 188--262 to 0--2 out of 300 per setting. No extracted span reaches 100 tokens, and the mean best recovery ratio ranges from 0.43\% to 6.45\%. Multilingual and related-code evaluations further show effective forgetting with limited impact on useful programming capabilities, supporting UNBIND as a practical approach to selective code unlearning.

</details>


### [104] [Evaluating Name-Only Directory Routing for One-Shot Code Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35918)
*Manoj Bajaj*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: 面向 one-shot 代码检索的目录名路由研究，直接针对 coding agent 早期文件定位与检索召回，含对照基线与成本分析。

TL;DR: 编码代理通过目录/文件名路由检索代码文件，在82个问题中八个候选内金标准文件召回0.465，优于FTS5的0.352和固定rg查询的0.245；结合FTS5达0.491但增益不确定，且无法归因于层次结构或证明提升问题解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定词法查询会遗漏相关代码文件，本文检验语言模型能否利用目录与文件名进行早期文件定位，以补充词法检索。

Method: 在11个仓库、82个修复前提交的审计问题上，比较仅名称目录路由、FTS5、固定全问题rg查询；在16K词元预算下评估标注行覆盖，并测试路由+FTS5、探索性扁平路径对照，统计召回、模型调用与耗时。

Result: 路由在八个候选内金标准文件召回0.465，高于FTS5 0.352和rg 0.245，配对增益0.113（95%仓库聚类自助法区间0.053–0.168）；在55个对齐标注案例中路由标注行0.443，FTS5为0.246；路由+FTS5文件召回0.491但增益不确定；扁平路径对照0.572但每问题24.6次模型调用；路由8.9秒/问题，FTS5构建0.9秒后7毫秒/查询。

Conclusion: 目录路由能为一次性词法搜索补充相关文件候选，但研究不能将增益归因于层次结构，也不能证明其改善问题解决。

中文摘要：找到正确的文件是编码代理早期面临的挑战。我们测试语言模型能否遵循目录和文件名，以找到固定词法查询所遗漏的带标注代码文件。在来自11个仓库、固定为修复前提交的82个经审计问题中，仅利用名称的目录路由在八个候选内召回了0.465的金标准文件，而FTS5为0.352，固定的全问题rg查询为0.245。相对于FTS5的配对增益为0.113（95%仓库聚类自助法区间，0.053至0.168）。在共享的16K词元上下文预算下，对于55个具有完全对齐标注的案例，路由交付了0.443的标注行，而FTS5为0.246。在相同的八文件限制下，将路由与FTS5结合达到0.491的文件召回率，但其相对于单独路由的增益不确定。一项探索性扁平路径对照达到0.572的召回率，同时每个问题使用24.6次模型调用，而路由为8.9次。路由平均每个问题耗时8.9秒；FTS5在0.9秒构建后每次查询耗时7毫秒。在该队列上，目录路由为一次性词法搜索添加了相关文件候选，但该研究无法将增益归因于层次结构，也无法表明它提高了问题解决率。

Original abstract: Finding the right files is an early challenge for coding agents. We test whether a language model can follow directory and file names to find annotated code files missed by fixed lexical queries. Across 82 audited issues from 11 repositories at pinned pre-fix commits, name-only directory routing recovered 0.465 of gold files within eight candidates, compared with 0.352 for FTS5 and 0.245 for a fixed full-issue rg query. The paired gain over FTS5 was 0.113 (95% repository-cluster bootstrap interval, 0.053 to 0.168). Under a shared 16K-token context budget, routing delivered 0.443 of annotated lines versus 0.246 for FTS5 on 55 cases with fully aligned annotations. At the same eight-file limit, combining routing with FTS5 reached 0.491 file recall, but its gain over routing alone was uncertain. An exploratory flat path control reached 0.572 recall while using 24.6 model calls per issue, compared with 8.9 for routing. Routing averaged 8.9 seconds per issue; FTS5 took 7 milliseconds per query after a 0.9-second build. On this cohort, directory routing added relevant file candidates to one-shot lexical search, but the study cannot attribute the gain to hierarchy or show that it improves issue resolution.

</details>


### [105] [From Dead Code and Static Requirements to Working Engines: Software Revival with Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36161)
*Tianyu Liu,Dingyuan Dai,Yufan Du,Zhen Yang*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: 14 pages, 3 figures

Selection reason: ReviveBench 用 coding agent 复活失效软件与从规范重建引擎，并审计执行式验证器缺陷，对 agent 长程任务与验证可靠性高度相关。

TL;DR: ReviveBench 以“复活”与“重建”两类任务族评估编码智能体，并通过审计揭示可执行验证本身会引入可观测量误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究编码智能体能否在保留原有方法的前提下让已失效软件重新可用，并依据公开规范重建工业软件引擎，同时检验用于衡量这些能力的验证器是否可靠。

Method: 构建 ReviveBench 基准，含复活（10 项）与重建（13 项）两类任务族；使用经原生执行环境、成熟工程工具或专门参考实现校准的隐藏验证器评分；开展标识符混淆的污染控制实验，并审计验证器缺陷，提出三项实用检查。

Result: 所有初始工作区均验证失败；最强模型在复活任务上每项至少一次运行通过全部十项，在后知识截止日期仓库的九次运行中通过八次；标识符混淆使相似度由 0.51–0.96 降至 0.03–0.44 而通过率未降；两个模型在重建任务全部十三项达标，但 CFD 任务无法证明数值求解器能力；审计发现 28 个验证器缺陷（24 个假阴性、2 个假阳性）。

Conclusion: ReviveBench 既评估软件复活与引擎重建，也提供了验证“用于测量编码智能体软件设计能力的验证器”的案例；可执行验证本身可能带来显著测量误差，应通过阈值可达性、候选一致性与产物重算诊断三项检查加以审视。

中文摘要：编码智能体能否在保留软件底层方法的前提下修复已无法运行的软件，并从公开规范出发重建工业软件引擎？在此，我们提出 ReviveBench，这是一个包含两类任务族的基准，其评估由隐藏验证器完成，而这些验证器以原生执行环境、成熟工程工具或专门构建的参考实现进行校准。“复活”（revival）任务族包含十项任务，涉及依赖不兼容、核心模块被删除、遗留构建以及一个基于 GPU 的基础模型。每个初始工作区都无法通过验证，而表现最强的受评模型在每项任务至少一次运行中通过了全部十项任务。在污染控制实验中，标识符混淆将与原实现的代码行相似度从 0.51–0.96 降至 0.03–0.44，且未降低任何受评模型的观测通过率。在声称的知识截止日期之后创建的代码仓库上，最强模型在九次运行中通过了八次。“重建”（reconstruction）任务族包含十三项任务，涵盖数值、几何、硬件与事务型系统（如 CAD 和 CRM）。有两个模型在全部十三项任务上都满足该基准的通过标准，尽管我们的审计表明，CFD 任务无法确立数值求解器能力。基准构建与审计共发现 28 个验证器缺陷，其中包括 24 个假阴性和 2 个假阳性。这些发现表明，可执行验证本身可能引入显著的测量误差。我们提出三项实用检查：检验规定的方法能否达到评分阈值，考察独立生成的候选结果之间的一致性，以及从提交的产物中重新计算诊断指标。因此，ReviveBench 既提供了对软件复活与引擎重建的评估，也提供了用于验证“测量编码智能体软件设计能力”的验证器的案例。

Original abstract: Can coding agents restore software that no longer runs while preserving its underlying methods, and reconstruct industrial software engines from open specifications? Here we introduce ReviveBench, a benchmark with two task families evaluated by hidden verifiers calibrated against native execution environments, established engineering tools, or purpose-built reference implementations. The revival family comprises ten tasks involving dependency incompatibilities, deleted core modules, legacy builds, and a GPU-based foundation model. Every starting workspace fails verification, and the strongest evaluated model passes all ten tasks in at least one run each. In contamination-control experiments, identifier obfuscation reduces line similarity to the original implementations from 0.51--0.96 to 0.03--0.44 without reducing the observed pass rate of any evaluated model. On repositories created after the stated knowledge cutoffs, the strongest model passes eight of nine runs. The reconstruction family comprises thirteen tasks spanning numerical, geometric, hardware, and transactional systems (e.g. CAD and CRM). Two models meet the benchmark's pass criteria on all thirteen, although our audit shows that the CFD task cannot establish numerical-solver capability. Benchmark construction and auditing uncover 28 verifier defects, including 24 false negatives and two false positives. These findings show that executable verification can itself introduce substantial measurement error. We present three practical checks: test whether prescribed methods can reach the grading thresholds, investigate agreement among independently generated candidates, and recompute diagnostics from submitted artifacts. ReviveBench thus provides both an evaluation of software revival and engine reconstruction, and cases in validating the verifiers used to measure coding agents for software design.

</details>


### [106] [Assay: Claims That Decay With the Code. Content-Addressed Evidence Graphs for Accountable AI-Assisted Software Delivery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36170)
*Om Shankar Tiwari,Tangi Vass,Gagan Deep Singh*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: 8 pages, 4 figures, 5 tables. Code, experiments, and paper source: https://github.com/OmShiv/assay-research

Selection reason: Assay 将 agent 声明绑定到代码依赖锥 Merkle 哈希，使证据随代码失效，配证据义务与无模型合并门，直接提升编码 agent 可信度与验证机制。

TL;DR: Assay 将智能体声称绑定到代码依赖锥的 Merkle 哈希，使声称随代码变更精确失效，并配合风险比例证据、职责分离评审和无模型合并门禁；在五个公共仓库上验证了上下文效率、重建速度、失效范围准确性与门禁对抗行为拦截效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 编码智能体在长上下文中重复定位代码，且困难时无证据宣称成功；现有仓库索引和评审编排分别解决但未统一，二者共享同一代码库结构对象。

Method: Assay 以依赖锥 Merkle 哈希绑定声称，证明绑定可靠且最小；在图上游施加风险比例证据义务、有界评审协议与职责分离、无模型合并门禁（覆盖率、新鲜度、签名、退出码、合理性、证据单调性、评审状态）；实现为无依赖 Python 工具与 MCP 服务器，并用已发布脚本生成全部数字。

Result: 在五个公共仓库上，600 token 简报成本比探索代理低 14x–114x；热重建比冷重建最多快 5x；锥绑定重验 7.9%–81.9% 声称，仓库绑定重验全部，按模块绑定遗漏 23%–68% 必需失效；门禁阻止 9/9 脚本化对抗行为并接纳诚实行为。

Conclusion: Assay 把当前代码事实与经谁在何修订验证的事实统一为可操作机制，实现精确失效追踪、风险匹配的评审与自动合并门禁，且全部结果可由发布脚本复现。

中文摘要：AI 编码智能体以两种相互耦合的方式失败。它们把大部分上下文窗口花在重新发现东西位于何处上，并且当工作变难时，它们在没有证据的情况下宣称成功。仓库索引以廉价上下文解决第一个问题，而带有对抗性评审的编排框架以问责制解决第二个问题。二者描述的是同一个对象，即代码库的结构，只是在两个时间尺度上：代码当前的真实情况，以及在哪个修订版、由谁验证过的真实情况。Assay 将这一观察付诸实践。智能体做出的每一条声称（测试通过、无秘密、行为保持不变）都绑定到其所覆盖代码的依赖锥的 Merkle 哈希，因此当该代码或其依赖的任何内容发生变化时，该声称恰好变为过时。我们证明这种绑定是可靠且最小的，并且变更的影响范围恰好是它所使其失效的声称集合。在该图上，我们施加与风险成比例的证据义务、一种带有职责分离的有界评审协议，以及一个不咨询任何模型的合并门禁：覆盖率、新鲜度、签名、退出码、合理性、证据单调性（“不要删除失败测试”的机械形式）和评审状态。Assay 是一个无依赖的 Python 工具，带有 MCP 服务器。在五个公共仓库上，一份 600 token 的简报成本比一个探索代理低 14 倍到 114 倍，热重建比冷重建最多快 5 倍，锥绑定重新验证 7.9% 到 81.9% 的声称，而仓库绑定会重新验证全部声称，同时按模块绑定会遗漏 23% 到 68% 的必需失效，并且该门禁在接纳诚实行为的同时阻止 9 个中的 9 个脚本化对抗行为。本文中的每个数字都由已发布的脚本生成。

Original abstract: AI coding agents fail in two coupled ways. They spend most of their context window rediscovering where things live, and they assert success without evidence when the work gets hard. Repository indexes address the first with cheap context, and orchestration frameworks with adversarial review address the second with accountability. Both describe the same object, the structure of the codebase, at two timescales: what is true of the code now, and what was verified to be true, at which revision, by whom. Assay makes that observation operational. Every claim an agent makes (tests pass, no secrets, behavior preserved) is bound to the Merkle hash of the dependency cone of the code it covers, so the claim is stale exactly when that code or anything it depends on changes. We show the binding is sound and minimal, and that the blast radius of a change is precisely the set of claims it invalidates. On the graph we place a risk-proportional evidence obligation, a bounded review protocol with separation of duties, and a merge gate that consults no model: coverage, freshness, signatures, exit codes, plausibility, evidence monotonicity (the mechanical form of "do not delete the failing test"), and review status. Assay is a dependency-free Python tool with an MCP server. On five public repositories a 600-token brief costs 14x to 114x less than an exploration proxy, warm rebuilds are up to 5x faster than cold ones, cone binding re-verifies 7.9% to 81.9% of claims where repository binding re-verifies all of them while per-module binding misses 23% to 68% of required invalidations, and the gate blocks 9 of 9 scripted adversarial behaviours while admitting the honest ones. Every number in this paper is generated by the released scripts.

</details>


### [107] [How Much Prompt Is Enough? A Blackbox Minimization of Few-Shots in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36289)
*Ali Alfageeh,Rahul Gopinath,Amin Alipour*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: 黑盒 few-shot 提示最小化框架，识别必需的结构化声明与冗余文本，可迁移到 agent 提示压缩与可靠性分析。

TL;DR: 提出黑盒提示最小化框架 \framework，将少样本提示压缩至必要子集，平均减少 65.3%±15.8% 字符且保持输出保真，并发现模型在提示编码/解码上的通用性差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提示对 LLM 行为至关重要，但其内部因果结构与冗余程度不透明，细微变化可能导致关键系统输出漂移，且缺乏提示可靠性分析手段。

Method: 提出黑盒提示最小化框架 \framework，将少样本提示缩减为必要的最小子集；通过案例研究将其应用于少样本学习系统，并进行实验分析。

Result: 少样本示例字符数平均减少 65.3%±15.8%，完全保持命题式输出保真度；模型偏好保留逻辑标识符与约束声明，丢弃自然语言叙述与跨提示关系注释；部分模型为通用编码器，部分为通用解码器。

Conclusion: 通过识别不可或缺的提示组件，\framework 为提示压缩和少样本示例的结构分析提供了原则性基础。

中文摘要：提示是引导大语言模型（LLM）行为的主要机制。然而，提示的内部结构和因果层级仍然鲜为人知：哪些部分在因果上是必要的、哪些部分是冗余的，仍是一个开放问题。这种不透明性可能带来严重后果。细微的提示变化可能悄无声息地改变关键软件系统中的模型输出，而工程师缺乏推断提示可靠性的技术。我们提出 \framework，一个黑盒提示最小化框架，可将少样本提示缩减为其必要的最小子集。我们通过一个案例研究将 \framework 应用于一个少样本学习系统，并展示该框架能够提供的洞见。我们的实验表明，少样本示例的字符数平均可减少 65.3% ± 15.8%，同时完全保持命题式输出保真度。模型优先保留逻辑标识符和约束声明，而丢弃自然语言叙述和跨提示关系注释。我们的分析还表明，一些模型是通用编码器，能够生成高度可读但已最小化的提示，而另一些模型是通用解码器，能够解释来自大多数其他模型的最小化提示。通过识别哪些组件不可或缺，\framework 为少样本示例的提示压缩和结构分析提供了有原则的基础。

Original abstract: Prompts are the primary mechanism for directing the behavior of large language models (LLMs). Yet the internal structure and causal hierarchy of prompts remain poorly understood: which parts are causally necessary and which are redundant is an open question. This opacity can have severe consequences. Subtle prompt variations can silently shift model outputs in critical software systems, and engineers lack techniques to reason about prompt reliability.
  We present \framework, a blackbox prompt-minimization framework that reduces few-shot prompts to their necessary minimal subset. We use a case study to apply \framework to a few-shot learning system and demonstrate the insights that this framework can provide.
  Our experiments show that few-shot exemplars can be reduced by a mean of 65.3\%~$\pm$~15.8\% in character count while fully preserving propositional output fidelity. The models preferentially retain logical identifiers and constraint declarations while discarding natural language prose and cross-prompt relational annotations.
  Our analysis also shows that some models are universal encoders, able to produce highly legible yet minimized prompts, while others are universal decoders, able to interpret minimized prompts from most other models.
  By identifying which components are indispensable, \framework provides a principled basis for prompt compression and structural analysis of few-shot exemplars.

</details>


### [108] [Strategies for Deploying AI Agents in Production at Scientific User Facilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36362)
*Ming Du,Xiangyu Yin,Michael Prince,Yi Jiang,Rajat Sainju,Tekin Bicer,Yanqi Luo,Eric Codrea,Peco Myint,Nina Andrejevic,Juanjuan Huang,Trupti Mohanty,Pawan Tripathi,Dishant Beniwal,Hemant Sharma,Doga Gursoy,Aileen Luo,Tao Zhou,Chenran Xu,Jan Ilavsky,Matthew T. Dearing,Ryan Chard,Hoon Seo,Dariusz Jarosz,Elaine Chandler,Thomas Fors,Hairong Shang,Pete Jemian,Xuli Wu,Madeline Miller,Ryan Aydelott,Nicholas Schwarz,Francesco de Carlo,Byeongdu Lee,Yine Sun,Moises Smart,Philippe Piot,Joe Sullivan,Mathew J. Cherukara,Alec Sandy,Stefan Vogt,Jonathan Lang,Laurent C. Chapon*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: 科学装置生产环境部署 LLM agent 的经验总结，涉 harness、工具服务器架构、可复用技能与治理，对工程化 agent 设计有参考价值。

TL;DR: 代理式AI正从研究演示走向科学用户设施的生产使用；基于APS的LLM代理部署，本文提炼可跨仪器和设施复用的设计原则与实用策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理式AI的科学价值超出吞吐量提升：它可承担校准、测量执行、质量控制等可重复任务及初步数据分析，使科学家聚焦假设、意外观察与解释；因此需要从实际部署中提炼可复用策略。

Method: 基于APS的LLM驱动代理部署经验，讨论光束线控制、设施知识检索和数据分析的代理框架，并提炼不依赖具体实现的底层设计原则，涵盖推理端点、工具服务器架构、非文本数据、计算密集型服务、可复用技能、受治理学习，以及网络与Linux运维、受治理共享内存和确定性编排。

Result: 提出一套可跨仪器和设施复用的实用策略与设计原则，并说明其可扩展到设施服务。由于LLM能力持续演进，这些建议代表截至封面日期该技术的快照。

Conclusion: 代理式AI可在科学设施生产环境中创造超越吞吐量的价值；通过模块化、可治理、可复用的设计原则，可跨仪器和设施部署，但需随LLM能力演进而持续更新。

中文摘要：代理式人工智能（AI）正超越研究演示，走向在科学用户设施中的生产使用，这些设施包括光源、中子源、纳米科学中心和自主实验室。其科学价值不止于提高吞吐量。代理可以执行校准、测量执行和质量控制中的可重复任务，以及将数据转化为可审查证据的初步分析，使科学家能够专注于假设、意外观察和解释。借鉴在APS部署LLM驱动代理的经验，本文提炼实用策略，重点强调可跨仪器和设施复用的要素。我们讨论用于光束线控制、设施知识检索和数据分析的代理框架，同时使底层设计原则独立于任何具体实现。这些原则涵盖推理端点、工具服务器架构、非文本数据、计算密集型服务、可复用技能，以及贯穿仪器生命周期的受治理学习。我们还考虑网络和Linux运维、受治理的共享内存以及确定性编排如何将这些模式扩展到设施服务中。由于LLM能力持续演进，这些建议代表了截至封面日期该技术的快照。
