Original abstract: Agentic artificial intelligence (AI) is moving beyond research demonstrations toward production use at scientific user facilities, including light sources, neutron sources, nanoscience centers, and autonomous laboratories. Its scientific value extends beyond increasing throughput. Agents can perform repeatable tasks in calibration, measurement execution, and quality control, as well as initial analyses that turn data into reviewable evidence, allowing scientists to focus on hypotheses, unexpected observations, and interpretation. Drawing on deployments of LLM-driven agents at the APS, this perspective distills practical strategies with an emphasis on elements that can be reused across instruments and facilities. We discuss agent harnesses for beamline control, facility knowledge retrieval, and data analysis while keeping the underlying design principles independent of any specific implementation. These principles cover inference endpoints, tool-server architectures, non-text data, computationally intensive services, reusable skills, and governed learning throughout an instrument's lifecycle. We also consider how network and Linux operations, governed shared memory, and deterministic orchestration can extend these patterns across facility services. Because LLM capabilities continue to evolve, these recommendations represent a snapshot of the technology as of the date on the cover.

</details>


### [109] [LatentSift: Policy-State Filtering for Token-Efficient Verification of Software Engineering Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36371)
*Yuning Han,Yangchenchen Jin,Tyler Jandreau,Jingwei Sun*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: LatentSift 用策略隐状态做无 token 无执行的候选过滤，在 SWE-bench 上大幅削减验证 token 并提升 Best@N，直接相关于代码 agent 的测试时扩展与验证。

TL;DR: LatentSift利用策略生成候选时的隐藏状态构建免token、免执行的过滤器，在SWE-bench Verified上大幅降低验证token并保持或提升Best@16性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时扩展带来的多候选生成与选择成本高，现有混合工作流中的LLM无执行验证器需对每条轨迹额外前向，导致token开销接近生成本身。

Method: LatentSift用策略生成候选时已产生的隐藏状态表征候选的推理、观察和函数调用状态，与训练中从成功/失败轨迹收集的正负状态库比较距离，再与学习到的线性分数融合，筛选进入执行阶段的有希望候选。

Result: 在SWE-bench Verified、三个智能体和两种策略规模下，K=16时EF验证器token减少66.6–81.0%，总验证token减少49.1–62.1%；混合Best@16匹配或优于各智能体参考工作流，DeepSWE-Preview从59.26%升至60.06%。

Conclusion: LatentSift表明可利用策略已有隐藏状态实现低成本候选筛选，在不牺牲甚至提升最终选择性能的同时显著降低验证开销。

中文摘要：测试时扩展通过生成多个候选轨迹并选择最佳轨迹来改进软件工程智能体。在这些长交互中进行验证和选择所消耗的token可能与生成本身一样多。现有混合工作流首先应用基于LLM的无执行（EF）验证器，在运行测试之前过滤候选，这会对每条轨迹额外增加一次模型前向传递。我们提出LatentSift，一种无需token且无需执行的过滤器，它用策略在生成候选时已经产生的隐藏状态替换这一第一阶段。它通过候选的推理、观察和函数调用状态来表示每个候选，将这些状态与在策略训练期间从成功和不成功轨迹中收集的此类状态的正样本库和负样本库进行比较，并将得到的距离分数与学习到的线性分数融合，以保留有希望的候选进入基于执行的阶段。在SWE-bench Verified上，在三个智能体和两种策略规模下，LatentSift在K=16时将EF验证器token减少66.6–81.0%，并将总验证token（包括测试生成）减少49.1–62.1%，同时混合Best@16匹配或优于每个智能体的参考工作流，在DeepSWE-Preview上从59.26%提升到60.06%。

Original abstract: Test-time scaling improves software engineering agents by generating multiple candidate trajectories and selecting the best one. Verifying and selecting among these long interactions can consume as many tokens as generation itself. Existing hybrid workflows first apply an LLM-based execution-free (EF) verifier to filter candidates before running tests, which adds another model pass over every trajectory. We introduce LatentSift, a token-free and execution-free filter that replaces this first stage with hidden states the policy already produces while generating the candidates. It represents each candidate through its reasoning, observation, and function-call states, compares them with positive and negative banks of such states collected from successful and unsuccessful trajectories during policy training, and fuses the resulting distance scores with a learned linear score to retain promising candidates for the execution-based stages. On SWE-bench Verified, across three agents and two policy sizes, LatentSift cuts EF-verifier tokens by 66.6--81.0% and total verification tokens, which include test generation, by 49.1--62.1% at K=16, while hybrid Best@16 matches or improves on each agent's reference workflow, rising from 59.26% to 60.06% on DeepSWE-Preview.

</details>


### [110] [WitnessGym: Benchmarking Coding Agents on the Construction of Bug Witnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36635)
*Haomin Qi,Xiangzhe Xu,Yiming Huang,Jingbo Shang,Chengpeng Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: 30 pages, 8 figures, and 12 tables, including appendices

Selection reason: WitnessGym 通过缺陷注入自动构建 1300 个 bug witness 基准，评测 coding agent 生成可执行复现证据的能力，直接相关。

TL;DR: WitnessGym 通过缺陷注入自动构建缺陷验证基准，生成 1,300 个 Java 用例；评估显示即使缺陷模式已知，可执行见证构建仍困难，且注入补丁与历史补丁难以区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缺陷验证产生的可执行证据能让审计发现可操作，但现有基准评估因用例复用公开历史缺陷和见证或需要人工构建而面临困难，因此需要自动化构建基准的方法。

Method: 提出 WitnessGym，向真实项目的测试可达路径注入缺陷，重新构建项目，并保留由构建时见证暴露的用例；通过缺陷规范和执行适配器支持扩展到其他缺陷类型和语言，并使用保留缺陷的变换改变周围结构。基于带有测试套件的真实 Java 项目自动构建 1,300 个基准用例。

Result: 注入的补丁类似历史缺陷补丁，两个受评估模型在盲法比较中难以区分；在四个编码智能体框架、六种框架/模型组合中，跨缺陷类型、执行上下文和变换深度评估，即使缺陷模式已知，见证构建仍然困难。

Conclusion: WitnessGym 能自动构建可扩展的缺陷验证基准，并表明可执行见证构建仍是挑战；框架、基准用例和评估脚本均可用。

中文摘要：缺陷验证要求编码智能体为所报告的缺陷生成一个可执行见证。该见证将具体输入与测试夹具相结合，并在执行期间暴露错误行为。此类证据使审计发现可操作，但当用例复用公开的历史缺陷和见证，或需要人工构建时，基准评估便很困难。我们提出 WitnessGym，一个通过缺陷注入构建缺陷验证基准的自动化框架。它向真实项目的测试可达路径注入缺陷，重新构建每个项目，并保留由构建时见证所暴露的用例。缺陷规范和执行适配器允许扩展到其他缺陷类型和语言。保留缺陷的变换在保持见证行为的同时改变周围结构。基于带有测试套件的真实 Java 项目，WitnessGym 自动构建了 1,300 个基准用例。注入的补丁与历史缺陷补丁相似，在盲法比较中，两个受评估模型难以区分它们。我们在六种框架/模型组合中评估了四个编码智能体框架，覆盖缺陷类型、执行上下文和变换深度。即使缺陷模式已知，见证构建仍然困难。我们的框架、基准用例和评估脚本均可用。

Original abstract: Bug validation asks a coding agent to produce an executable witness for a reported bug. The witness combines a concrete input with a testing harness and exposes faulty behavior during execution. Such evidence makes audit findings actionable, yet benchmark evaluation is difficult when cases reuse public historical bugs and witnesses or require manual construction. We present WitnessGym, an automated framework for constructing bug-validation benchmarks through bug injection. It injects bugs into test-reached paths of real projects, rebuilds each project, and retains cases exposed by a construction-time witness. Bug specifications and execution adapters allow extension to additional bug types and languages. Bug-preserving transformations vary the surrounding structure while preserving the witness behavior. Based on real-world Java projects with test suites, WitnessGym automatically constructs 1,300 benchmark cases. The injected patches resemble historical bug patches and are difficult for the two evaluated models to distinguish in blinded comparisons. We evaluate four coding agent frameworks in six framework/model pairings across bug types, execution contexts, and transformation depths. Witness construction remains difficult even when the bug pattern is known. Our framework, benchmark cases, and evaluation scripts are available.

</details>


### [111] [CTE-Bench: Counterfactual Trace Evaluation for Stateful Software Simulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36647)
*Xinran Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: Accepted at NeurIPS 2026 (Track on Evaluations and Datasets). Dataset: https://huggingface.co/datasets/zhangxr7/cte-bench-core-v1

Selection reason: CTE-Bench 以反事实轨迹评测模型对带状态服务干预后果的预测，含记忆协议与可执行 oracle，服务 agent 状态推理与评测。

TL;DR: CTE-Bench 评估模型能否在编码代理干预有状态服务后预测未来响应；提供正确历史反馈时效应步匹配率为 54.3%–61.5%，隐藏反馈或依赖自生成预测时降至约 23%–33%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 函数级代码执行基准忽略持久化服务状态，代理基准只评分动作或最终状态，无法衡量干预后未来行为的预测能力，而错误预期可能延迟多次调用才暴露。

Method: 构建 CTE-Bench：每个场景给出 Python 服务代码、干预前调用与响应、干预（源码编辑或状态覆写）及 40 个未来调用；模型预测全部未来响应，并由执行服务校验。三种记忆协议分别提供正确早期响应、不提供响应、提供模型自身早期预测。CTE-Bench-Core-v1 含 255 个场景、六个确定性 Python 服务、每模型 10,200 条预测；主指标为 effect-step VM，即干预改变响应的 2,476 个未来调用上的精确匹配率。

Result: 揭示正确早期响应时，四个 API 托管模型达到 54.3%–61.5% effect-step VM；隐藏早期响应降至 23.2%–28.9%；以自生成预测为条件为 24.8%–33.2%；端到端完全正确预测的场景至多 1.2%。

Conclusion: 当前模型主要在获得正确反馈时才能跟踪干预效应，且错误会在 rollout 中累积。作者发布 CTE-Bench-Core-v1、可执行 oracle、评估脚本和评估卡。

中文摘要：编码代理会改变正在运行的软件：它们修补服务的代码或覆写其存储状态，然后依据自身对服务之后将如何响应的预期来行动。一个错误的预期可能直到若干次调用之后才显现。函数级代码执行基准忽略了持久化服务状态，而代理基准则对代理采取的动作或其最终达到的状态进行评分。我们提出 CTE-Bench，它衡量模型能否预测某项干预如何改变有状态服务的未来行为，而不要求它选择动作。每个场景向模型提供 Python 服务代码、干预前观察到的调用与响应、干预本身（源代码编辑或状态覆写）以及 40 个固定的未来调用；模型预测每一个未来响应，并通过执行服务来检查预测。三种记忆协议控制模型看到的是正确的早期响应、完全看不到它们，还是看到自己早先的预测。CTE-Bench-Core-v1 包含覆盖六个确定性 Python 服务的 255 个场景，每个模型产生 10,200 条预测。主评分指标是效应步值匹配（effect-step value match，VM）：在 2,476 个其响应因干预而改变的未来调用上实现精确响应相等。在揭示正确早期响应的情况下，四个 API 托管模型（DeepSeek V4-Flash、Kimi K2.5、Qwen3.6-35B-A3B 和 Claude Sonnet 4.6）达到 54.3%–61.5% 的效应步 VM。隐藏这些响应会将效应步 VM 降至 23.2%–28.9%；以自生成预测为条件则得到 24.8%–33.2%，并且至多 1.2% 的场景被端到端完全正确预测。因此，当前模型主要在提供正确反馈时才能跟踪干预效应，且其错误会在一次 rollout 中累积。我们发布 CTE-Bench-Core-v1，附带可执行 oracle、评估脚本，以及将每项主张映射到其协议的评估卡。

Original abstract: Coding agents change running software: they patch a service's code or overwrite its stored state, and then act on their own expectation of how the service will respond afterwards. A wrong expectation may surface only several calls later. Function-level code-execution benchmarks omit persistent service state, and agent benchmarks score the actions an agent takes or the final state it reaches. We introduce CTE-Bench, which measures whether a model can predict how an intervention changes a stateful service's future behavior, without asking it to choose actions. Each scenario gives the model Python service code, the calls and responses observed before the intervention, the intervention itself (a source edit or a state overwrite), and 40 fixed future calls; the model predicts every future response, and predictions are checked by executing the service. Three memory protocols control whether the model sees the correct earlier responses, none of them, or its own earlier predictions. CTE-Bench-Core-v1 contains 255 scenarios over six deterministic Python services, giving 10,200 predictions per model. The main score is effect-step value match (VM): exact response equality on the 2,476 future calls whose response the intervention changes. With correct earlier responses revealed, four API-hosted models (DeepSeek V4-Flash, Kimi K2.5, Qwen3.6-35B-A3B, and Claude Sonnet 4.6) reach 54.3%-61.5% effect-step VM. Hiding those responses lowers effect-step VM to 23.2%-28.9%; conditioning on self-generated predictions gives 24.8%-33.2%, and at most 1.2% of scenarios are predicted exactly end to end. Current models thus track intervention effects mainly when correct feedback is supplied, and their errors compound over a rollout. We release CTE-Bench-Core-v1 with its executable oracle, evaluation scripts, and an evaluation card mapping each claim to its protocol.

</details>


### [112] [The Editor Has Read-Only Access: Correctness Signals in Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36783)
*Angad Miglani,Samrath Singh Chadha,Kevin Li,Manas Venkata Sai Ravulapalli*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: 探测扩散语言模型内部激活是否编码代码正确性并尝试引导生成，对代码正确性信号检测与可控生成有可迁移价值。

TL;DR: 扩散语言模型的内部激活可用线性探针区分代码正确性，但探针点估计相较模型置信度无一致优势，沿探针方向引导未能可靠提升生成性能，反向引导反而降低性能；该结果不等同于统计显著性结论或普遍不可引导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究扩散语言模型在生成代码时，其内部激活是否编码代码正确性信息，并检验能否利用该信息改进代码生成。

Method: 在六个扩散模型上训练线性探针区分通过/失败尝试；用小幅语义突变作对照；与模型置信度比较；将探针导出方向加入残差流进行引导测试，并测试相反方向；辅以补充方法、存档结果和代码记录干预与统计校准/可复现性限制。

Result: 线性探针能区分通过和失败尝试，最强读取通常出现在早期层之后；语义突变对照支持与正确性相关而非仅表层风格；与模型置信度相比，探针点估计无一致优势；正向引导未带来可靠改进，反向引导降低性能。

Conclusion: 扩散语言模型的内部激活包含与代码正确性相关的信息，但在所测试设置中，探针点估计和探针方向引导未能可靠转化为生成性能提升；需区分上述观察与统计显著性主张或模型普遍不可引导的结论，并注意统计校准与可复现性局限。

中文摘要：扩散语言模型通过反复更新部分掩码的序列来生成代码。我们探究其内部激活是否编码代码正确性，以及该信息能否改进生成。在六个扩散模型中，线性探针能够区分通过尝试与失败尝试，最强的读取通常出现在早期层之后。使用小规模语义突变的对照支持其与正确性相关，而不仅仅是表层风格。在与模型置信度的比较中，探针点估计未表现出一致优势。将探针导出的方向添加到残差流中，在测试的引导设置下并未带来可靠改进，而相反方向会降低性能。我们将这些观察与关于统计显著性或一般无法引导的说法区分开来。补充方法、存档结果和代码记录了所测试的干预措施及其统计校准与可复现性的局限。

Original abstract: Diffusion language models generate code by repeatedly updating a partially masked sequence. We ask whether their internal activations encode code correctness and whether that information can improve generation. Across six diffusion models, linear probes distinguish passing from failing attempts, with the strongest reads generally appearing beyond the early layers. Controls using small semantic mutations support a connection to correctness rather than surface style alone. In comparisons with model confidence, probe point estimates offer no consistent advantage. Adding a probe-derived direction to the residual stream does not yield a dependable improvement in the tested steering settings, while the opposite direction degrades performance. We distinguish these observations from claims about statistical significance or a general inability to steer. Supplementary methods, archived results, and code document the tested interventions and the limits of their statistical calibration and reproducibility.

</details>


### [113] [XRepoSkill: Learning Transferable Skills for Software Engineering Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36807)
*Yaoqi Guo,Haoyang Zhou,Jiayi Zhang,Yiran Zhang,Yang Liu,Qiuyuan Chen,Qiang Lin,Hande Dong,Jie M. Zhang,Zhenpeng Chen*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: 从成功/失败轨迹对比中抽取可跨仓库迁移的规则型技能，并用于 SWE-bench 等仓库级任务，直接对应 coding agent 的技能学习与长程执行。

TL;DR: XRepoSkill 通过对比同一问题上的成功与失败轨迹，从执行路径分歧中提取可执行规则，并验证其与成功解决相关且跨仓库复现，从而学习可迁移技能；在 SWE-bench Pro 和 DeepSWE 上，它在六种基准—LLM 组合中均取得最佳问题解决率，并在 DeepSWE 上显著超过无技能智能体和最强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件工程智能体虽可从既往经验中提炼可复用技能，但这些技能常常难以跨仓库迁移；关键难点在于成功轨迹中的行为未必是成功的原因，可能有用、偶然或只是模型习惯。

Method: 提出 XRepoSkill 基于轨迹学习可迁移技能：将技能表示为规则集合，规定动作及触发时机；对比同一智能体在同一问题上的成功与失败轨迹，从路径分歧处生成候选规则；为每条规则配备可执行谓词以在其他轨迹上系统评估；根据规则与成功问题解决的关联进行验证，仅保留在多个仓库中重复出现的行为，并合并仓库特定变体；对新问题选择相关规则引导智能体。

Result: 从 SWE-bench Verified 官方排行榜公开发布轨迹中学习技能，在 SWE-bench Pro 和 DeepSWE 上使用来自不同厂商的三个骨干 LLM 评估，且评估仓库均不在技能学习轨迹池中；与三种近期技能学习方法相比，XRepoSkill 在所有六个基准—LLM 组合中问题解决率最高；在 DeepSWE 上，相比同一智能体无学习技能提升 10.3 个百分点，相比最强技能学习基线提升 5.0 个百分点。

Conclusion: XRepoSkill 能有效从轨迹中学习可迁移技能，提升跨仓库问题解决性能，并在多个基准和 LLM 组合中优于现有技能学习方法。

中文摘要：软件工程智能体日益使用从先前经验中提炼的可复用技能来解决仓库级问题，然而此类技能往往无法跨仓库迁移。一个核心挑战是，成功轨迹中出现的行为不一定对成功结果负责：它可能确实有用，可能只是偶然附带，也可能只是模型的重复性习惯。我们提出 XRepoSkill，一种基于轨迹学习可迁移技能的方法。我们将技能表示为一组规则，每条规则规定在问题解决过程中采取什么动作以及何时采取该动作。XRepoSkill 首先对比同一智能体在同一问题上的成功与失败轨迹，并从它们执行路径发生分歧之处推导候选规则。每条规则与一个可执行谓词配对，使得其规定行为可在其他轨迹上被系统评估。规则根据其与成功问题解决的关联进行验证，并且仅当其规定行为在多个仓库中重复出现时才被保留；随后，同一行为的仓库特定变体被合并为可迁移规则。对于新问题，XRepoSkill 选择相关规则来引导智能体。我们从官方 SWE-bench Verified 排行榜上公开发布的轨迹中学习技能，并使用来自不同厂商的三个骨干 LLM 在 SWE-bench Pro 和 DeepSWE 上评估；评估仓库均不出现在技能学习轨迹池中。与三种近期技能学习方法相比，XRepoSkill 在所有六个基准—LLM 组合中取得最高问题解决率。特别是在具有挑战性的长时程 DeepSWE 基准上，XRepoSkill 相比没有学习技能的同一智能体将问题解决率提升 10.3 个百分点，相比最强技能学习基线提升 5.0 个百分点。

Original abstract: Software engineering agents increasingly use reusable skills distilled from prior experience to resolve repository-level issues, yet such skills often fail to transfer across repositories. A central challenge is that a behavior appearing in a successful trajectory is not necessarily responsible for the successful outcome: it may be genuinely useful, merely incidental, or simply a recurring habit of the model. We introduce XRepoSkill, a trajectory-based approach for learning transferable skills. We represent a skill as a collection of rules, each specifying what action to take and when to take it during issue resolution. XRepoSkill first contrasts successful and failed trajectories of the same agent on the same issue and derives candidate rules from where their execution paths diverge. Each rule is paired with an executable predicate that enables its prescribed behavior to be evaluated systematically on other trajectories. A rule is verified based on its association with successful issue resolution and retained only when its prescribed behavior recurs across multiple repositories; repository-specific variants of the same behavior are then consolidated into transferable rules. For a new issue, XRepoSkill selects relevant rules to guide the agent. We learn skills from publicly released trajectories on the official SWE-bench Verified leaderboard and evaluate them on SWE-bench Pro and DeepSWE using three backbone LLMs from different vendors; none of the evaluation repositories appears in the skill-learning trajectory pool. Against three recent skill learning methods, XRepoSkill achieves the highest issue resolution rate in all six benchmark--LLM combinations. In particular, on the challenging long-horizon DeepSWE benchmark, XRepoSkill improves issue resolution by 10.3 percentage points over the same agent without learned skills and by 5.0 points over the strongest skill-learning baseline.

</details>


### [114] [GitCF: Reducing Incomplete Changes by Exploiting Multiple Similarities Among Commits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37045)
*Yuto Kitabayashi,Takashi Kobayashi*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: 利用 commit 元素集合与文本（消息、diff、issue）双重相似度做漏改位置推荐，可作为代码 agent 变更定位/补全检索的参考。

TL;DR: 提出 GitCF，通过结合修改元素集合与提交文本内容（提交消息、代码差异、关联问题描述）比较当前变更与历史变更，从而推荐额外修改位置；在543个不完整变更上优于三种协同变更规则基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发者难以找全变更影响位置，遗漏会导致不完整变更。现有协同变更规则方法无法推荐极少协同变更的元素且忽略提交文本；另一类利用文本的方法多不使用提交历史，也不以开发者当前变更为输入。

Method: GitCF 将开发者当前变更与修订历史中的过往变更进行比较，融合两类相似性：被修改元素集合的相似性，以及提交文本内容（提交消息、代码差异、关联 issue 描述）的相似性，据此推荐额外变更位置。

Result: 在17个开源项目的543个不完整变更上评估，与三种基于协同变更规则的技术对比。GitCF 在 MAP、Recall@10、Recall@20、Hit@10、Hit@20 五项指标上均优于最强基线；Hit@10 从0.322提升至0.403，平均精度提升统计显著。

Conclusion: 结合协同变更信息与提交文本信息能更有效推荐遗漏的变更位置，缓解不完整变更问题。

中文摘要：软件开发人员常常难以识别其变更所影响的所有位置，不完整的变更往往源于这些遗漏。为缓解该问题，已有若干方法被提出，用于推荐应与开发者当前变更一起修改的额外位置。其中一大类技术从代码库修订历史中挖掘协同变更规则，但由于它们仅依赖哪些元素曾被一起修改，因此无法推荐极少发生协同变更的元素，并且忽略了随提交附带的文本信息。第二类技术确实利用了文本信息，但其文本来自源代码或变更请求，而非提交历史，并且不以开发者当前变更为输入。为解决这些局限，我们提出 GitCF，一种变更推荐方法，它将开发者当前变更与修订历史中的过往变更进行比较，并通过结合两种相似性来源来推荐额外的变更位置：被修改元素的集合以及提交的文本内容，包括提交消息、代码差异和关联的问题描述。我们在来自17个开源项目的543个不完整变更上评估 GitCF，其中不完整变更指某一提交中遗漏的修改在后续提交中被补上；并将 GitCF 与三种基于协同变更规则的技术进行比较。GitCF 在所有五项指标（MAP、Recall@10、Recall@20、Hit@10 和 Hit@20）上均优于最强基线；特别地，它将 Hit@10 从 0.322 提高到 0.403，并且平均精度的提升具有统计显著性。

Original abstract: Software developers often struggle to identify all locations that their changes affect, and incomplete changes frequently result from these omissions. To mitigate this problem, several approaches have been proposed to recommend additional locations that should be modified together with a developer's current change. A large class of these techniques mines co-change rules from repository revision histories, but because they rely solely on which elements have been modified together, they cannot recommend elements that have rarely co-changed, and they disregard the textual information that accompanies commits. A second class of techniques does exploit textual information, but it derives it from the source code or from a change request rather than from commit history, and it does not use the developer's current change as its input. To address these limitations, we propose GitCF, a change recommendation method that compares a developer's current change with past changes in the revision history and recommends additional change locations by combining two sources of similarity: the set of modified elements and the textual content of commits, including commit messages, code diffs, and linked issue descriptions. We evaluate GitCF on 543 incomplete changes from 17 open-source projects, where an incomplete change denotes a commit in which an omitted modification is supplied in a later commit, comparing it against three co-change-rule-based techniques. GitCF outperforms the strongest baseline on all five metrics (MAP, Recall@10, Recall@20, Hit@10, and Hit@20); in particular, it raises Hit@10 from 0.322 to 0.403, and the improvement in average precision is statistically significant.

</details>


### [115] [LoLBench: Evaluating Coding Agents with Long-Horizon Proposals on Large Software Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37143)
*Yun Peng,Zihan Wu,Zeyang Zhuang,Xin Zhou,Rui Shu,Xu Han,Chun Yong Chong,Yuan Wang,Jiakun Liu*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: 面向大型软件系统的长程提案到实现评测基准，揭示代码定位瓶颈并验证给定文件树可显著提升解决率，对 agent 评测与规划高度相关。

TL;DR: LoLBench是一个面向大型软件系统、覆盖提案到实现全过程的多语言基准，包含100个任务、29个系统、五个领域，用于评估编码代理的感知与实现能力。最佳代理仅解决14%任务，F2P通过率52.7%；代码定位不完整是主要瓶颈，加入参考衍生文件树和API规格可将解决率提升至最高34%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码代理基准多关注根据详细规格生成代码编辑的实现能力，但实际模块化开发还需要从用户意图和高层设计中推导规格说明的感知能力。为同时评估感知与实现，需要覆盖大型软件系统完整提案到实现过程的基准。

Method: 提出LoLBench，一个多语言基准，包含来自五个领域、29个软件系统的100个任务。每个任务给出人工编写的增强提案，含用户意图和高层设计。平均提案约5,000词，系统约240万LoC，实现PR约更改5,500 LoC。评估28个代理，并分析失败原因及提供参考衍生文件树与API规格的影响。

Result: 28个代理中最佳代理仅解决14%任务，F2P通过率52.7%。失败分析表明不完整代码定位是主要瓶颈；提供参考衍生文件树与API规格说明可将解决率提高16–22个百分点（2.4–17倍），最高达到34%。

Conclusion: 在大型软件系统的实际模块化开发中，感知与实现仍是编码代理的核心挑战。LoLBench公开于https://huggingface.co/datasets/lolbench26/LoLBench。

中文摘要：现代编码代理能够交付越来越大规模的仓库级更改，近期的基准测试也通过强调包含大型参考实现的长期任务来反映这一点。许多基准测试评估编码代理根据详细规格说明生成正确代码编辑的实现能力。然而，实际的模块化开发任务还要求具备感知能力，即锚定用户意图和高层设计以推导出规格说明。我们提出 LoLBench，通过大型软件系统上的完整提案到实现过程来评估这两种能力。它是一个多语言基准，包含来自五个领域、29个软件系统的100个任务。每个任务都提供一份由人工编写的增强提案，其中包含用户意图和高层设计。平均而言，提案约含5,000词，软件系统约含240万源代码行（LoC），实现拉取请求（PR）约更改5,500 LoC。在我们评估的28个代理中，最佳代理仅解决14%的任务，并达到52.7%的Fail-to-Pass（F2P）通过率。失败分析发现，不完整的代码定位是主要瓶颈；而提供由参考实现衍生的文件树以及API规格说明，可将解决率提高16–22个百分点（2.4–17倍），最高达到34%。这些结果表明，在大型软件系统上的实际模块化开发中，感知和实现仍然是编码代理的核心挑战。LoLBench可在https://huggingface.co/datasets/lolbench26/LoLBench获取。

Original abstract: Modern coding agents can deliver increasingly large repository-level changes, and recent benchmarks reflect this by emphasizing long-horizon tasks with large reference implementations. Many benchmarks evaluate coding agents' implementation capability to produce correct code edits from detailed specifications. However, practical modular development tasks also require the perception capability of grounding user intent and high-level design to derive a specification. We introduce LoLBench to evaluate both capabilities through the entire proposal-to-implementation process on large software systems. It is a multilingual benchmark of 100 tasks across 29 software systems in five domains. Each task provides a human-written enhancement proposal with user intent and high-level design. On average, proposals contain about 5,000 words, software systems contain 2.4 million source lines of code (LoC), and implementation pull requests (PRs) change approximately 5,500 LoC. Across 28 agents we evaluated, the best agent resolves only 14% of tasks and achieves a 52.7% Fail-to-Pass (F2P) pass rate. Failure analysis identifies incomplete code localization as a major bottleneck, while providing reference-derived file trees alongside API specifications improves resolved rates by 16--22 percentage points (2.4--17$\times$), reaching at most 34%. These results show that both perception and implementation remain central challenges for coding agents in practical modular development on large software systems. LoLBench is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/lolbench26/LoLBench.

</details>


### [116] [CRJudgeBench: Can AI Detect Plausible but Invalid Code Reviews?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37216)
*Yue Pan,Jiawei Li,Ziyuan Zhang,Xiangxin Zhao,He Ye*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: 26 pages, 5 figures, and 14 tables. Under review at ICLR 2027. Dataset available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/dcloud347/CRJudgeBenchmark

Selection reason: 代码评审评论可信度判定基准与仓库取证式 agentic judge，属于代码审查/验证类 agent 的方法与评测，可迁移性强。

TL;DR: 论文提出代码审查评论技术可信度判断任务，构建 CRJudgeBench 基准，并提出基于仓库证据收集的智能体评判器 Sentinel，通过迭代动作级学习显著提升判断准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码审查基准主要关注评论生成、问题发现或一般质量，缺少对单条审查评论技术可信性的直接评估；同时，大语言模型生成的评论可能看似合理但技术错误，会误导开发者。

Method: 构建 CRJudgeBench，包含 1199 个来自真实拉取请求和专家验证扰动的实例，覆盖可信与不可信评论；提出 Sentinel，一种基于仓库的智能体式评判器，主动收集代码证据验证评论；从 Qwen3-Coder-30B-A3B-Instruct 出发，通过特权教师的迭代动作级学习在训练集上训练。

Result: 在 359 个实例的测试集上，Sentinel 达到 76.60% 准确率，比 GLM-5.3 高 6.13 个百分点，比基础模型高 19.78 个百分点。

Conclusion: 即使最先进的通用大语言模型也难以识别不可信评论，而迭代动作级学习可显著提升基于仓库的可信度判断准确性。数据集已公开。

中文摘要：大型语言模型能够生成看似合理的代码审查评论，但这些评论可能包含技术上不正确的论断，从而误导开发者。我们研究技术可信度判断：确定某条审查评论的核心技术论断是否正确，并且在考虑其仓库上下文的情况下是否适用于被审查的代码。现有的代码审查基准主要评估审查生成、问题发现或一般评论质量，但没有直接评估智能体能否判断单条审查评论在技术上的可信性。为填补这一空白，我们引入 CRJudgeBench，一个包含 1199 个实例的基准，由真实拉取请求和专家验证的扰动构建，覆盖可信评论和看似合理但不可信的评论。我们进一步提出 Sentinel，一种基于仓库的智能体式评判器，在做出判断之前主动收集代码证据以验证审查评论。从 Qwen3-Coder-30B-A3B-Instruct 出发，Sentinel 通过来自特权教师的迭代动作级学习，在 CRJudgeBench 训练划分上进行训练。在 359 个实例的 CRJudgeBench 测试集上，Sentinel 达到 76.60% 的准确率，比 GLM-5.3 高出 6.13 个百分点，比其基础模型高出 19.78 个百分点。这些结果表明，即使是最先进的通用大型语言模型也难以识别不可信的评论，而迭代动作级学习显著提高了基于仓库的可信度判断的准确性。我们的数据集可在 https://huggingface.co/datasets/dcloud347/CRJudgeBenchmark 获取。

Original abstract: Large language models can generate plausible code-review comments, but such comments may contain technically incorrect claims that mislead developers. We study technical trustworthiness judgment: determining whether a review comment's core technical claims are correct and applicable to the reviewed code in its repository context. Existing code-review benchmarks primarily evaluate review generation, issue discovery, or general comment quality, but do not directly assess whether an agent can determine the technical trustworthy of an individual review comment. To fill this gap, we introduce CRJudgeBench, a benchmark of 1199 instances constructed from real pull requests and expert-verified perturbations, covering both trustworthy and plausible but untrustworthy comments. We further present Sentinel, a repository-grounded agentic judge that actively gathers code evidence to verify review comments before making judgments. Starting from Qwen3-Coder-30B-A3B-Instruct, Sentinel is trained on the CRJudgeBench training split through iterative action-level learning from a privileged teacher. On the 359-instance CRJudgeBench test set, Sentinel achieves 76.60\% accuracy, outperforming GLM-5.3 by 6.13 percentage points and its base model by 19.78 points. These results show that even state-of-the-art general-purpose LLMs struggle to identify untrustworthy comments, while iterative action-level learning substantially improves the accuracy of repository-grounded trustworthiness judgments. Our dataset is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/dcloud347/CRJudgeBenchmark

</details>


### [117] [SafeLLM4SE: Statistical Evaluation and Reporting for LLM-based Software Engineering Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37294)
*Francisco Ortin*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: This work has been submitted for possible publication. Copyright may be transferred without notice, after which this version may no longer be accessible

Selection reason: 针对 LLM 软件工程系统的统计评估与最小报告标准（自适应采样、置信区间、效应量），可迁移到 agent 优化效果的可复现评测。

TL;DR: 提出 SafeLLM4SE，将 LLM 生成输出视为随机过程，提供统计评估方法与最低报告标准，并开源为 PyPI 软件包，在 HumanEval 上比较两个 LLM 进行示例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 在软件工程任务中的随机行为挑战评估的有效性、可重复性和可比性；单一输出、平均分、best-of-N 或 pass@k 等传统报告方式会掩盖变异性和估计不确定性，可能导致对系统可靠性的误导性结论。

Method: 提出 SafeLLM4SE 方法论和报告标准：不将输出视为确定性产物，而视为随机过程实现，区分质量、稳定性与估计不确定性；结合自适应采样与置信区间、分布感知统计比较、效应量，以及涵盖模型配置、可重复性、评估程序和资源使用的最低报告标准；并发布 PyPI 开源软件包。

Result: 提供可在 PyPI 获取的开源软件包，支持复现和扩展该方法论；通过在 HumanEval 上比较两个 LLM 展示其应用。

Conclusion: SafeLLM4SE 为基于 LLM 的软件工程系统提供统计上原则化的评估与报告方式，有助于提升评估有效性、可重复性与可比性，并减少因随机性和估计不确定性导致的误导性可靠性结论。

中文摘要：大型语言模型（LLMs）正越来越多地用于软件工程任务，但其随机行为对其评估的有效性、可重复性和可比性构成了挑战。传统做法，如报告单一输出、平均分数、best-of-N 或 pass@k 性能，可能掩盖变异性和估计不确定性，从而可能导致关于系统可靠性的误导性结论。本文提出 SafeLLM4SE，一种用于对基于 LLM 的软件工程系统进行统计上原则化评估的实用方法论和报告标准。SafeLLM4SE 不将生成的输出视为确定性产物，而是将其视为随机过程的实现，并区分质量、稳定性和估计不确定性。它结合了自适应采样与置信区间、分布感知的统计比较、效应量，以及涵盖模型配置、可重复性、评估程序和资源使用的最低报告标准。SafeLLM4SE 还以开源软件包形式提供，可在 PyPI 上获取，使研究人员和实践者能够复现和扩展该方法论。我们通过比较两个 LLM 在 HumanEval 上的应用来说明其应用，HumanEval 是一个通过功能测试评估的编程问题基准。

Original abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for software engineering tasks, yet their stochastic behavior challenges the validity, reproducibility, and comparability of their evaluations. Conventional practices such as reporting a single output, an average score, best-of-N, or pass@k performance can obscure variability and estimation uncertainty, potentially leading to misleading conclusions about system reliability. This article presents SafeLLM4SE, a practical methodology and reporting standard for statistically principled evaluation of LLM-based software engineering systems. Rather than treating generated outputs as deterministic artifacts, SafeLLM4SE treats them as realizations of a stochastic process and distinguishes quality, stability, and estimation uncertainty. It combines adaptive sampling with confidence intervals, distribution-aware statistical comparisons, effect sizes, and a minimum reporting standard covering model configuration, reproducibility, evaluation procedures, and resource usage. SafeLLM4SE is also provided as an open-source software package available on PyPI, enabling researchers and practitioners to reproduce and extend the methodology. We illustrate its application by comparing two LLMs on HumanEval, a benchmark of programming problems assessed through functional tests.

</details>


### [118] [Do Agent Benchmarks Do What They Say? An Executable-Contract Audit of Tool-Using Agent Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37315)
*Rohith Reddy Bellibatlu,Zichong Wang,Wenbin Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: 10 pages. Submitted to IEEE BigData 2026, Intelligent Data Mining special session. Code, contracts and data: https://github.com/rohithreddybc/tool-contract-conformance

Selection reason: 以可执行契约审计工具型 agent 基准，发现工具实现与接口不一致导致分数失真，对 agent 评测可靠性与验证机制有直接借鉴。

TL;DR: 论文审计工具型智能体基准中的工具契约，发现模拟工具报告与接口宣称/实现不符，导致评分基于缺陷工具本应写入的状态；在四个基准34个变更型工具中确认7个工具缺陷和1个评估器属性，并揭示检查器静态部分主导发现、动态部分确认追踪，以及AgentDojo、tau2-bench和临床基准中的具体评分失真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具型智能体在错误动作有真实成本的环境中部署，但认证基准仅依据模拟工具调用的报告评分，默认工具执行了接口宣称的行为；审计分类法缺少该假设类别，缺陷会在每次重跑中持续影响分数。

Method: 将工具宣称表面视为可执行契约，对照实现进行检查；通过任务文件和评估器代码追溯分数来源至由缺陷工具本应写入状态导出的判定；在四个基准的34个变更型工具及AgentDojo 25工具变更面上审计，并用注入缺陷、阴性对照和路径构造评估检查器。

Result: 固定提交确认7个工具缺陷和1个评估器属性；注入缺陷测试中25个标记无误报、5个阴性对照标记2个、多数漏检（33个计分漏检中29个有条款覆盖但无探针揭示）；静态部分单独标记17个确认位点中的14个；AgentDojo 25工具面至少5个工具偏离宣称表面；无黄金轨迹触及tau2-bench缺陷，1,120条路径上评估器奖励对暂停线路充值却使修复工具失败；临床基准中评分器把工具消息当证据，动作成功率只反映请求是否携带预期载荷而非记录是否改变。

Conclusion: 现有工具型智能体基准可能因工具实现违背接口契约而产生系统性评分失真；审计需同时检查工具实现与评估器，并区分发现与确认/追踪；仅依赖工具报告和动作成功率会高估或误判智能体表现。

中文摘要：使用工具的智能体正在进入错误动作会带来真实成本的环境，而用于认证它们的基准评估的是每个模拟工具调用报告自己做了什么，并假设工具执行了其接口所宣称的行为。我们调查的审计分类法没有为这一假设设立类别，而分数之下的缺陷在每次重跑时都存在。我们将工具对外宣称的表面视为可执行契约，对照它检查实现，并追溯每个分数的来源，经由任务文件和评估器代码，一直追溯到由有缺陷工具本应写入的状态所导出的判定。在四个基准中的34个受审计变更型工具上，我们在固定提交处确认了7个工具缺陷和1个评估器属性。对于注入缺陷，检查器在25个标记中未产生假阳性，标记了5个阴性对照中的2个，并漏检了大多数：在33个计分漏检中的29个里，某个条款覆盖了该缺陷，但没有探针将其揭示出来。检查器自身的静态部分单独运行时，标记了17个确认位点中的14个，因此在这些发现上，动态部分起确认和追踪作用，而非发现作用。另外12个AgentDojo工具，连同6个留出工具和最先审计的7个工具，构成其25个变更型工具的完整表面；按照我们的契约解读，该表面上至少有5个工具偏离其宣称表面，这是仅就AgentDojo而言的比率。没有任何黄金轨迹触及两个tau2-bench缺陷中的任何一个；在1,120条为隔离该电信缺陷而构建的路径上（该数目由构造固定），评估器奖励对一条已暂停线路的充值，却让已修复的工具失败。最清晰的案例是一个临床基准，其工具告知智能体每次写入都已执行，而这是在一种有文档记录但接口未披露的无写入设计下进行的；其评分器将该消息当作证据，因此其动作成功率记录的是请求是否携带预期载荷，而不是任何记录是否发生了改变。

Original abstract: Tool-using agents are entering settings where a wrong action carries real cost, and the benchmarks certifying them grade what each simulated tool call reports having done, assuming the tool did what its interface advertises. The audit taxonomies we survey publish no category for that assumption, and a defect beneath a score is present on every rerun. We treat a tool's advertised surfaces as an executable contract, check the implementation against it, and trace each score's provenance through the task files and evaluator code to the verdicts that derive from state a defective tool should have written. Across 34 audited mutating tools in four benchmarks we confirm seven tool defects and one evaluator property at pinned commits. On injected defects the checker raised no false positive in 25 flags, flagged 2 of 5 negative controls, and missed most: in 29 of 33 scored misses a clause covered the defect but no probe revealed it. The checker's own static half, run alone, flags 14 of 17 confirmed sites, so on these findings the dynamic half confirms and traces rather than discovers. Twelve further AgentDojo tools, with six held-out tools and the seven audited first, complete its 25-tool mutating surface, on which at least 5 tools diverge from their advertised surface as our contracts read it, a rate for AgentDojo alone. No gold trajectory reaches either tau2-bench defect; on 1,120 paths built to isolate the telecom defect, a number fixed by construction, the evaluator rewards a refuel of a suspended line and fails the repaired tool. The clearest case is a clinical benchmark whose tool tells the agent each write executed under a documented no-write design its interface does not disclose; its grader takes that message as evidence, so its action success rate records whether a request carried the expected payload, not whether any record changed.

</details>


### [119] [Complexity-Aware Evaluation of LLM Comprehension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37405)
*Ali Mohammadi Esfahani,Nafiseh Kahani,Samuel A. Ajila*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: 按圈复杂度、嵌套深度等度量分层评估 LLM 代码理解可靠性，为代码 agent 的能力诊断式评测提供可迁移维度。

TL;DR: 论文提出复杂度感知框架，用圈复杂度、嵌套深度、分支因子和Halstead体积评估LLM代码理解；DeepSeek-Coder-V2与Llama在自动输入-输出预测和人工语义理解上均随代码复杂度升高而显著下降，说明聚合准确率会掩盖可靠性退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM已广泛用于代码理解相关软件工程任务，但聚合基准准确率可能掩盖模型可靠性随源代码结构复杂度增加而发生的变化，因此需要更细粒度的诊断性评估。

Method: 构建复杂度感知评估框架，采用圈复杂度、嵌套深度、分支因子和Halstead体积四类指标；评估DeepSeek-Coder-V2和Llama；任务包括300个Python函数的自动输入-输出预测，以及60个函数平衡子集的人工语义理解评估；函数按低、中、高复杂度分组，并使用相关性和逻辑回归分析复杂度与正确性的关系。

Result: DeepSeek-Coder-V2总体自动准确率为78.33%，Llama为70.33%；从低到高复杂度，DeepSeek-Coder-V2准确率由93.52%降至52.78%，Llama由87.04%降至47.22%；错误预测与四个复杂度指标的更高值一致相关；人工语义理解也呈相同退化模式，DeepSeek-Coder-V2由100.00%降至75.00%，Llama由90.00%降至60.00%。

Conclusion: 复杂度感知评估比单独使用聚合准确率更能诊断LLM代码理解可靠性；随着源代码结构复杂度增加，模型正确率显著下降，因此评估应纳入复杂度维度。

中文摘要：大型语言模型（LLMs）越来越多地用于需要理解现有源代码的软件工程任务，包括行为预测、函数解释、调试和代码审查。然而，聚合基准准确率可能掩盖模型可靠性如何随源代码在结构上变得更加复杂而变化。本文提出一个复杂度感知框架，使用圈复杂度、嵌套深度、分支因子和Halstead体积来评估LLM代码理解。我们通过两个互补任务评估DeepSeek-Coder-V2和Llama：在300个Python函数上进行自动输入-输出预测，以及在60个函数的平衡子集上进行人工评估的语义理解。这些函数被分组为低、中、高复杂度区间。DeepSeek-Coder-V2达到78.33%的总体自动准确率，而Llama为70.33%。然而，准确率从低复杂度到高复杂度大幅下降：DeepSeek-Coder-V2从93.52%降至52.78%，Llama从87.04%降至47.22%。错误预测始终与全部四个复杂度指标的更高值相关，相关性和逻辑回归分析证实结构复杂度与正确性之间存在大致可比的负相关。人工语义理解显示出相同的退化模式，DeepSeek-Coder-V2的准确率从100.00%降至75.00%，Llama从90.00%降至60.00%。这些发现表明，复杂度感知评估比单独的聚合准确率能提供对LLM代码理解可靠性更具诊断性的评估。

Original abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for software engineering tasks that require understanding existing source code, including behavior prediction, function explanation, debugging, and code review. However, aggregate benchmark accuracy can conceal how model reliability changes as source code becomes structurally more complex. This paper presents a complexity-aware framework for evaluating LLM code comprehension using cyclomatic complexity, nesting depth, branching factor, and Halstead volume. We evaluate DeepSeek-Coder-V2 and Llama through two complementary tasks: automatic input-output prediction over 300 Python functions and manually assessed semantic comprehension over a balanced subset of 60 functions. The functions are grouped into Low-, Medium-, and High-complexity bands. DeepSeek-Coder-V2 achieves an overall automatic accuracy of 78.33%, compared with 70.33% for Llama. However, accuracy decreases substantially from Low to High complexity, from 93.52% to 52.78% for DeepSeek-Coder-V2 and from 87.04% to 47.22% for Llama. Incorrect predictions are consistently associated with higher values of all four complexity metrics, and correlation and logistic-regression analyses confirm broadly comparable negative associations between structural complexity and correctness. Manual semantic comprehension shows the same degradation pattern, with accuracy decreasing from 100.00% to 75.00% for DeepSeek-Coder-V2 and from 90.00% to 60.00% for Llama. These findings demonstrate that complexity-aware evaluation provides a more diagnostic assessment of LLM code-comprehension reliability than aggregate accuracy alone.

</details>


### [120] [Profiling the Energy Consumption of Serverless Functions with Joule Profiler](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37531)
*Jérémy Woirhaye,François Gibier,Anderson Andrei Da Silva,Francescomaria Faticanti,Thomas Ledoux,Romain Rouvoy*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: 15 pages, 6 figures, 5 tables

Selection reason: 系统化 profiling/benchmark 1401 种 serverless 配置的能耗与性能，其分层基准实验方法对性能/能耗调优有参考价值。

TL;DR: 本文通过基准测试 1401 种无服务器函数配置，实证分析其能效，并给出部署配置选择优先级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多将无服务器函数视为黑盒，只关注平台性能，缺乏对打包为无服务器函数后执行环境各层如何影响能效的关键认识。

Method: 设计可探索无服务器函数执行各层影响的实验基准测试环境，评估 9 种执行环境、7 种语言运行时配置、11 种工作负载和 3 种输入规模组合而成的 1401 种配置，回答三个研究问题。

Result: 结果表明，配置选择应优先考虑编程语言，其次是语言运行时，最后才是执行环境；执行环境仅对短生命周期函数重要，且其最佳选择取决于运行时。

Conclusion: 无服务器函数能效优化需按语言、运行时、执行环境的顺序进行权衡；相关基准环境、实验工件、原始测量和分析代码已公开。

中文摘要：云提供商和客户已广泛采用无服务器计算作为按需部署和执行函数的便捷范式。为此，无服务器平台需要在函数代码的哪怕一行运行之前，配置合适的执行环境。这些环境由若干层组成，例如容器引擎、虚拟机监控器（hypervisors）、unikernels 以及编程语言运行时。尽管文献已经研究了这些无服务器平台的性能，但它们将函数视为黑盒，社区缺乏关于将应用打包为无服务器函数的环境影响的关键洞察。因此，本文对可部署在无服务器平台上的无服务器函数的能效进行了实证研究。我们设计了一个实验基准测试环境，使利益相关者能够探索执行无服务器函数所涉及的各个层的影响。我们使用该环境评估了 1,401 种配置，结合了 9 种执行环境、7 种语言运行时配置、11 种工作负载和 3 种输入规模，以回答三个研究问题：无服务器函数最流行的编程语言是否最具能效？哪些因素对其能效影响最大？部署它们的最具能效的配置是什么？我们的结果表明，应首先选择编程语言，然后选择语言运行时，最后才选择执行环境；执行环境仅对短生命周期函数重要，且其最佳选择取决于运行时。我们的基准测试环境、实验工件、原始测量数据和分析代码均已公开可用。

Original abstract: Cloud providers and customers have widely adopted serverless computing as a convenient paradigm for deploying and executing functions on demand. To do so, serverless platforms require provisioning an appropriate execution environment before a single line of the function's code runs. These environments consist of several layers, such as container engines, hypervisors, unikernels, and programming language runtimes. While the literature has investigated the performance of these serverless platforms, it treats functions as black boxes, and the community lacks key insights into the environmental impacts of packaging applications as serverless functions. This paper therefore empirically studies the energy efficiency of serverless functions deployable on serverless platforms. We design an experimental benchmarking environment that lets stakeholders explore the impacts of the various layers involved in executing serverless functions. We use it to evaluate 1,401 configurations, combining 9 execution environments, 7 language-runtime configurations, 11 workloads, and 3 input sizes, to answer three research questions: Are the most popular programming languages for serverless functions the most energy-efficient? What factors most affect their energy efficiency? What are the most energy-efficient configurations to deploy them? Our results show that one should first choose the programming language, then the language runtime, and only then the execution environment, which matters only for short-lived functions and whose best choice depends on the runtime. Our benchmarking environment, experimental artifacts, raw measurements, and analysis code are publicly available.

</details>


### [121] [Independent Verification Paths Are Not Independent: A Case Study of Common-Mode Failure in a Satellite Catalogue Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37603)
*Fabio Rovai*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: Accepted at the AI for Science workshop (NeurIPS 2026). Code, prompts, raw model outputs and per-trial records: https://github.com/fabio-rovai/space-object-register-ontology (paper/gates/), archived at https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.22002834

Selection reason: 揭示冗余独立验证路径因共享常量导致的共模失效，且 LLM 生成的独立检查大多复现错误，对 agent 验证/反思机制设计有警示价值。

TL;DR: 冗余计算门控在跨目录完整性研究中失效：两条路径共享对状态词汇的误读常量，形成共模错误；更正版也含伪影，模型独立检查仍大量复现缺陷计数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据流水线常用不同技术路径的冗余计算作为保障，本文报告这种保障在跨目录完整性研究中失败，揭示共同模式错误与语义错误可绕过冗余验证。

Method: 对两个地球轨道物体开放登记册进行跨目录完整性研究；比较基于集合的 Python 路径与 RDF 图上的 SPARQL 查询；建立对象级账本核对数字；用三项回到源文档的检查测试缺陷代码与更正版；再用对象相位历史核对更正；最后以三个固定模型、禁用工具进行受控复现，生成独立检查路径。

Result: 门控在七项计数上打印所有交叉检查一致，但三项错误，其中一项从 220 高估到 932；两条路径共享误读来源状态词汇的常量，故错误为共模。更正版仍有 42/261 处不一致为伪影，且三项检查均未发现。受控复现中，75 条独立检查路径有 72 条算出缺陷计数，即使提示含来源自身代码定义也有 29/30 条如此。

Conclusion: 该证据表明冗余计算只验证了实现，未能验证含义；到达发表阶段的错误是含义错误，因此需要回到来源文档和未被流水线读取的源数据，对语义解释进行独立检查。

中文摘要：数据流水线的一种常见保障是冗余计算：通过基于不同技术的两条路径推导每个已发布数字，并在它们不一致时拒绝退出。我们报告这样一个门控在一个跨目录完整性研究中失效，该研究涉及两个地球轨道物体的开放登记册。一个比较基于集合的 Python 路径与对输出的 RDF 图进行 SPARQL 查询的门控，在七项计数上打印了“ALL CROSS-CHECKS AGREE”。其中三项是错误的，一项高估超过四倍（932 对 220）。两条路径都导入了相同的常量，这些常量编码了对来源状态词汇的误读，因此错误是共模的，门控无法发现它。我们给出了机制、一个核对每个数字的对象级账本，以及三项回到来源文档的检查，并在有缺陷的代码及其更正版上进行了测量。随后，我们对照每个对象的相位历史检查了该更正，这些历史保存在流水线从未读取的源文件中。该更正同样是错误的：在其 261 处不一致中，有 42 处是伪影，而我们的三项检查均未标记出它们。最后，在一项使用三个固定模型并禁用工具的受控复现中，按要求生成的作为独立检查的 75 条路径中有 72 条计算出了有缺陷的计数；即使提示中携带了来源自身对这些代码的定义，30 条中仍有 29 条如此。证据来自一个流水线和一个缺陷族。在其中，冗余验证的是实现，而到达发表阶段的错误是含义错误。

Original abstract: A common safeguard for a data pipeline is redundant computation: derive each published number by two routes built on different technology and refuse to exit when they disagree. We report one such gate failing, in a cross-catalogue integrity study of two open registers of Earth-orbiting objects. A gate comparing a set-based Python path with SPARQL queries over the emitted RDF graph printed ALL CROSS-CHECKS AGREE on seven counts. Three were wrong, one overstated more than fourfold (932 against 220). Both paths imported the same constants, which encoded a misreading of the source's status vocabulary, so the error was common-mode and the gate could not see it. We give the mechanism, an object-level ledger reconciling every figure, and three checks that go back to the source's documentation, measured on the defective code and on its correction. We then checked that correction against each object's phase history, held in a source file the pipeline never read. The correction was also wrong: 42 of its 261 disagreements are artefacts, and none of our three checks flagged them. Finally, in a controlled replication with three pinned models and tools disabled, 72 of 75 paths generated on request as independent checks computed the defective count, 29 of 30 even when the prompt carried the source's own definitions of the codes. The evidence is one pipeline and one defect family. Within it, redundancy verified implementation, and the errors that reached publication were errors of meaning.

</details>


### [122] [Retrieve, Reproduce, Reveal: Dissecting Retrieval-Augmented Software Vulnerability Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37669)
*Sabrina Kaniewski,Tim Krämer,Julius Bächle,Markus Enzweiler,Michael Menth,Tobias Heer*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: 对六个开源 RAG 漏洞检测系统做复现与组件级分解评估，指出检索有效不等于检测可靠，可迁移到代码 agent 的 RAG 流程拆解与评测。

TL;DR: 本文研究六个开源RAG4SVD系统，针对专有模型、异构数据集/知识库/模型/指标导致的可复现性与可比性问题，在开放权重设置下复现实验并构建统一基准，同时开展组件级分析。结果显示可复现性差异大，已发表性能难以在受控开放权重评估中迁移，且检测效果高度依赖模型；有效RAG4SVD依赖流水线阶段对齐，检索有效未必带来可靠检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG4SVD评估多依赖专有模型，且数据集、知识库、骨干模型和指标不一致，导致开放科学、可复现性和跨系统比较困难；同时系统多组件却常仅端到端评估。

Method: 研究六个开源RAG4SVD系统；（i）在开放权重设置下复现其实验设置；（ii）使用共同数据集、指标套件和开放权重模型池构建统一基准；（iii）将代表性RAG4SVD流水线分解为输入抽象、知识检索和检测进行组件级分析。

Result: 可复现性因系统差异显著；在统一基准的受控开放权重评估下，已发表RAG4SVD性能不能迁移，且强依赖所用模型。组件分析显示有效RAG4SVD依赖流水线阶段对齐；例如oracle知识使检索接近最优，但性能仍低（0.51成对准确率），说明仅检索有效不足以可靠检测。

Conclusion: 应不仅在端到端层面，也在流水线组件层面评估RAG4SVD，并推动更标准化、RAG感知的评估实践。

中文摘要：检索增强生成（RAG）越来越多地用于增强基于大型语言模型（LLM）的软件漏洞检测，通过将预测建立在检索到的漏洞知识（如漏洞报告）之上。然而，现有的基于RAG的软件漏洞检测（RAG4SVD）系统通常使用专有模型进行评估，这对开放科学和可复现性提出了挑战。此外，研究使用不同的数据集、自定义知识库、不同的骨干模型和多样的指标，这阻碍了有意义的跨系统比较。在本工作中，我们研究了六个开源RAG4SVD系统，并通过以下方式应对这些可复现性和可比性挑战：（i）在开放权重设置下复现其实验设置，以及（ii）使用共同数据集、指标套件和开放权重模型池进行统一基准测试。此外，RAG4SVD系统通常由多个组件组成，但往往仅作为整体系统（即端到端）进行评估。因此，我们进行（iii）组件级分析，将代表性的RAG4SVD流水线分解为输入抽象、知识检索和检测。我们的结果表明，可复现性因系统而异且差异显著。在所提出的统一基准下，已发表的RAG4SVD性能在受控的开放权重评估中并不能迁移，并且强烈依赖于所使用的模型。组件分析表明，有效的RAG4SVD取决于流水线阶段之间的一致性。例如，oracle知识将检索提升至接近最优，但性能仍然较低（0.51成对准确率），表明仅靠检索有效性不足以实现可靠检测。这些发现促使我们不仅在端到端层面评估RAG4SVD，而且在流水线组件层面进行评估，并为更标准化、具有RAG意识的评估实践提供了基础。

Original abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) is increasingly used to enhance Large Language Model (LLM)-based software vulnerability detection by grounding predictions in retrieved vulnerability knowledge, such as vulnerability reports. However, existing RAG-based software vulnerability detection (RAG4SVD) systems are often evaluated using proprietary models, which challenges open science and reproducibility. Further, studies use different datasets, custom knowledge bases, different backbone models, and diverse metrics, which hinders meaningful cross-system comparison. In this work, we study six open-source RAG4SVD systems and address these reproducibility and comparability challenges through (i) reproduction of their experimental settings under an open-weight setting, and (ii) a unified benchmark using a common dataset, metric suite, and pool of open-weight models. Further, RAG4SVD systems typically consist of multiple components, yet are often evaluated only as a whole system, i.e., end-to-end. Therefore, we perform (iii) a component-level analysis that decomposes representative RAG4SVD pipelines into input abstraction, knowledge retrieval, and detection. Our results demonstrate that reproducibility varies substantially across systems. Under the presented unified benchmark, published RAG4SVD performance does not transfer under a controlled open-weight evaluation and depends strongly on the used model. The component analysis shows that effective RAG4SVD depends on the alignment between pipeline stages. For example, oracle knowledge raises retrieval to near-optimal, yet performance remains low (0.51 pairwise accuracy), demonstrating that retrieval effectiveness alone is insufficient for reliable detection. These findings motivate evaluating RAG4SVD not only end-to-end, but at the level of pipeline components, and provide a basis for more standardized, RAG-aware evaluation practices.

</details>


### [123] [Is manual software optimization a thing of the past?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37849)
*Pavlin G. Poličar,Martin Špendl,Tomaž Hočevar*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: LLM agent 自主优化科学软件性能，含低层优化与算法重构、最高两个数量级加速并经维护者验证，与 SemOpt 主题直接吻合。

TL;DR: LLM智能体可自主优化科学软件，在t-SNE、ssGSEA和图元计数上超越现有最快工具，最高提速两个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学软件需处理更大数据，传统优化依赖深厚专家知识；LLM进展提供自动化可能，需验证智能体能否优化成熟软件。

Method: 人类定义范围、正确性标准和验证机制，LLM智能体自主优化三个计算问题的软件，维护者审查并验证正确性。

Result: 所有测试配置中优化实现均更快，最高比现有最快工具快两个数量级；改进包括底层代码优化、数学重构和新图元计数算法。

Conclusion: 软件优化可委托给自主智能体，人类角色转向选题、定义目标、提供验证和确保正确性；对范围明确可验证问题，手动优化或成过去。

中文摘要：科学软件越来越需要在保持可接受执行时间的同时处理更大的数据集。软件优化传统上要求编程、算法和数值方法方面的深厚专业知识。近期大语言模型（LLM）的进展为自动化这一过程的大部分提供了可能。我们研究基于LLM的智能体能否在科学软件中自主实现显著性能提升，包括已由人类开发者广泛优化的成熟实现。我们让一个基于LLM的智能体优化三个计算问题的软件：t-SNE、单样本基因集富集分析（ssGSEA）和图元计数（graphlet counting）。人类定义了范围、正确性标准和验证机制，之后智能体自主工作，在某些情况下持续数小时。代码维护者审查了每个生成的实现并验证其正确性。在所有测试配置中，优化后的实现都更快，比现有最快工具最高快两个数量级。这些改进包括底层代码优化、数学重构，以及一种全新的图元计数算法。软件优化可以越来越多地委托给自主智能体，人类的角色从实现优化转向决定优化哪些软件、定义目标、提供验证机制，并确保最终软件的正确性。对于范围明确、可验证的问题，我们认为手动软件优化可能已成为过去。

Original abstract: Scientific software is increasingly required to process larger datasets while maintaining acceptable execution times. Software optimization traditionally requires substantial expertise in programming, algorithms, and numerical methods. Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) offer the possibility of automating much of this process. We investigate whether LLM-based agents can autonomously achieve substantial performance improvements in scientific software, including mature implementations that have already been extensively optimized by human developers. We tasked an LLM-based agent with optimizing software for three computational problems: t-SNE, single-sample gene set enrichment analysis (ssGSEA), and graphlet counting. Humans defined the scope, correctness criteria, and a verification mechanism, after which the agent worked autonomously, in some cases for several hours. Code maintainers reviewed each resulting implementation and verified its correctness. The optimized implementations were faster in all tested configurations, by up to two orders of magnitude over the fastest existing tools. The improvements included low-level code optimizations, mathematical reformulations, and an entirely new algorithm for graphlet counting. Software optimization can increasingly be delegated to autonomous agents, with the human role shifting from implementing optimizations to deciding which software to optimize, defining objectives, providing verification mechanisms, and ensuring the correctness of the final software. For well-scoped, verifiable problems, we argue that manual software optimization may be a thing of the past.

</details>


### [124] [AgentBug-Smith: Automatically Reproducing Real-World Harness Bugs in Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37864)
*Yiming Cheng,Alfin Wijaya Rahardja,Mengshi Zhang,Zihao Chen,Zhenpeng Chen,Yiling Lou*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: 20 pages, 8 figures. Code: https://github.com/EaminC/AgentBug-Smith Data: https://huggingface.co/buckets/EaminChan/live-harness-bench

Selection reason: 自动复现 agent harness 真实缺陷并构建可扩展基准，还从修复中蒸馏可复用修复技能，直接服务 agent 可靠性提升与自改进。

TL;DR: 提出 AgentBug-Smith 自动复现真实世界 agent harness 缺陷，并构建包含 200 个可复现缺陷的动态基准 Live-Harness-Bench，用于评估和改进软件智能体的 harness 缺陷修复能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent harness 缺陷修复困难，而现有基准规模小、固定且构建成本高，阻碍了该领域进展。

Method: AgentBug-Smith 从开源智能体系统中持续发现并复现真实世界 harness 缺陷；并据此构建可扩展基准 Live-Harness-Bench，用于评估与知识蒸馏。

Result: AgentBug-Smith 在不同骨干 LLM 上比通用软件缺陷复现技术成功率提高 10.67%–27.56%；Live-Harness-Bench 目前含 200 个可复现缺陷；评估显示先进智能体修复能力有限；蒸馏修复技能使修复率提高 6.32%。

Conclusion: AgentBug-Smith 与 Live-Harness-Bench 提供可扩展基础，将真实失败转化为可执行评估实例和可复用知识，推动递归自我改进智能体的发展。

中文摘要：智能体测试框架（harness）缺陷展现出独特的特征，并且对于最先进的软件智能体而言仍然难以修复。该领域的进展进一步受到现有基准的阻碍：这些基准仅包含少量且固定数量的可执行 harness 缺陷，同时需要数百小时的人工构建。本工作提出了 AgentBug-Smith，一种自动化的 harness 缺陷复现方法，能够持续从开源智能体系统中发现并复现真实世界的 harness 缺陷。在不同骨干 LLM 上，AgentBug-Smith 始终优于为通用软件设计的现有缺陷复现技术，在复现 harness 缺陷的成功率上提高 10.67%–27.56%。通过将 AgentBug-Smith 应用于实际开源智能体系统，我们构建了 Live-Harness-Bench，一个动态且可扩展的基准，目前包含 200 个可复现的 harness 缺陷。我们进一步通过两个下游应用展示了 Live-Harness-Bench 的效用。首先，我们将 Live-Harness-Bench 用作评估基准，以系统评估最先进的软件智能体，揭示其在修复真实世界 harness 缺陷方面的能力有限。其次，我们将 Live-Harness-Bench 用作真实世界 harness 缺陷修复的知识库，从中可以蒸馏出可复用的修复技能，以改进现有软件智能体，将其 harness 缺陷修复率提高 6.32%。AgentBug-Smith 与 Live-Harness-Bench 共同为在 harness 缺陷修复方面持续评估和改进软件智能体建立了可扩展的基础，将真实世界的智能体失败转化为可执行的评估实例和用于 harness 改进的可复用知识，从而助力实现递归自我改进智能体的最终目标。

Original abstract: Agent harness bugs exhibit unique characteristics and remain challenging for state-of-the-art software agents to repair. Progress in this area is further hindered by existing benchmarks, which contain only a small and fixed number of executable harness bugs while requiring hundreds of human hours to construct. This work presents AgentBug-Smith, an automated harness bug reproduction approach that continuously discovers and reproduces real-world harness bugs from open-source agentic systems. Across different backbone LLMs, AgentBug-Smith consistently outperforms existing bug reproduction techniques designed for general software, achieving 10.67% - 27.56% higher success rates of reproducing harness bugs. By applying AgentBug-Smith to open-source agentic systems in the wild, we construct Live-Harness-Bench, a live and extensible benchmark that currently contains 200 reproducible harness bugs. We further demonstrate the utility of Live-Harness-Bench through two downstream applications. First, we use Live-Harness-Bench as the evaluation benchmark to systematically evaluate state-of-the-art software agents, revealing their limited capabilities in repairing real-world harness bugs. Second, we use Live-Harness-Bench as a knowledge base of real-world harness bug fixes, from which reusable repair skills can be distilled to improve existing software agents, increasing their harness-bug repair rates by 6.32%. Together, AgentBug-Smith and Live-Harness-Bench establish a scalable foundation for continuously evaluating and improving software agents on harness bug repair, turning real-world agent failures into executable evaluation instances and reusable knowledge for harness improvement, thus contributing to the ultimate goal of recursively self-improving agents.

</details>


### [125] [Merged, Not Measured: An Empirical Study of Performance Issues Fixed by Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37985)
*Zhenyu Qi,Haotang Li,Jinfu Chen,Huashan Chen,Yutong Zhao,Derui Zhu,Tomas Cerny,Bo Liu,Sen He*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: 大规模实证研究 coding agent 提交的性能修复 PR，重执行验证其加速声明是否成立，涵盖性能问题修复、测试与验收判定，高度相关。

TL;DR: 研究分析了 1,262 个由编码代理修复的性能问题，发现合并结果更多取决于代理与仓库的历史交互，而非修复内容；许多合并修复未达性能声称。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码代理提交声称加速软件的 PR，但维护者如何回应以及这些性能声称是否成立尚不清楚，且人类性能修复研究对此提供的信息有限。

Method: 从 AIDev v4 的 71,677 个代理 PR 出发，结合文本过滤与语言模型、作者进行的编码簿编码，筛选出六个代理在 582 个仓库中修复的 1,262 个性能问题；对问题及测试进行编码，并重新执行 23 个被拒绝和 30 个被合并的修复。

Result: 57% 的已关闭修复被合并，61% 的拒绝未说明原因，23 个被拒绝修复重新执行后仅 6 个成立；接受率随代理在仓库中的历史记录（31–37% 到 70%）和仓库此前对其他代理 PR 的合并率（33% 到 84%）上升；合并修复删除更改行数比例更高（0.26 vs 0.15），但编码内容、描述、测试或测量无此差异；44% 的问题由重复计算和冗余数据处理导致，46% 的修复为架构级；37% 修复更改测试，11% 含性能测试或基准；30 个合并修复中 18 个达标、3 个未达声称、9 个无显著提升或回归、14 个在未测试输入上改变行为。

Conclusion: 修复结果与仓库和该代理的历史记录相关，而非与修复的编码内容相关；合并并不证明修复实现了其性能声称。

中文摘要：编码代理会提交声称能加速软件的拉取请求（PR），但关于人类性能修复的研究很少说明维护者如何回应此类修复，或该声称是否成立。从 AIDev v4 的 71,677 个代理 PR 中，通过文本过滤以及语言模型和作者进行的编码簿编码，选出了 1,262 个由六个代理在 582 个仓库中修复的性能问题。我们对每个问题及其测试进行编码，并重新执行 23 个被拒绝的修复和 30 个被合并的修复。（1）57% 的已关闭修复被合并，61% 的拒绝未给出明确原因，而在主要基于代理构建的工作负载上，我们的三次运行试点中，23 个重新执行的被拒绝声称中仅有 6 个成立。（2）接受率随着该代理在该仓库中的历史记录（从 31–37% 升至 70%）以及该仓库在开启该 PR 之前对其他代理 PR 的合并率（从 33% 升至 84%）而上升。已合并的修复删除了其所更改行数的更大比例（0.26 对 0.15），这一差异在代理内部和仓库内部均成立，而在编码内容、描述、测试或测量中未检测到此类差异。（3）重复计算和冗余数据处理导致了 44% 的问题，46% 的修复是架构层面的。（4）代理在 37% 的修复中更改了测试，11% 带有性能测试或基准；在 30 个已合并修复中，18 个满足我们的交付标准，3 个未达到声称，9 个未显示显著提升或出现回归，14 个在未测试输入上改变了行为。结果跟随的是仓库与该代理的历史，而非修复的编码内容，并且合并并不表明该修复实现了其声称的效果。

Original abstract: Coding agents open pull requests (PRs) that claim to speed up software, but studies of human performance fixes say little about how maintainers respond to such a fix or whether its claim holds. From the 71,677 agent PRs of AIDev v4, a text filter and codebook coding by language models and by the authors select 1,262 performance issues fixed by six agents in 582 repositories. We code each issue and its tests and re-execute 23 rejected and 30 merged fixes. (1) 57% of closed fixes are merged, 61% of rejections give no stated reason, and only 6 of the 23 re-executed rejected claims held under our three-run pilot on mostly agent-built workloads. (2) Acceptance rises with the agent's track record in the repository (31-37% to 70%) and with the repository's pre-opening merge rate on its other agent PRs (33% to 84%). Merged fixes delete a larger share of the lines they change (0.26 versus 0.15), a difference that holds within agent and within repository, with no such difference detected in the coded content, description, tests or measurements. (3) Repeated computation and redundant data processing cause 44% of the issues, and 46% of fixes are architectural-level. (4) Agents change tests in 37% of fixes and 11% carry a performance test or benchmark; of the 30 merged fixes, 18 met our delivery criterion, 3 fell short of the claim, 9 showed no significant gain or regressed, and 14 change behavior on untested inputs. The outcome tracks the repository's history with the agent rather than the coded content of the fix, and a merge does not show that the fix delivers what it claims.

</details>
